FUNCTION makeCode
LINES 30175-44615
TERMS dash-priority, dash-kpi, vx-group, vx-jetzt, renderDashboard

function makeCode(code, label, isRecommended) {
    var filled = code.replace('{NR}', nr);
    var safeCode = filled.replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/&/g, '&amp;');
    // tel:-Encoding: * → %2A, # → %23
    var telHref = 'tel:' + filled.replace(/\*/g, '%2A').replace(/#/g, '%23');
    var blockClass = isRecommended
      ? 'setup-code-block setup-code-block--recommended'
      : 'setup-code-block';
    var chip = isRecommended
      ? '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;'
          + 'background:rgba(5,150,105,0.18);color:#34D399;border:1px solid rgba(5,150,105,0.35);'
          + 'border-radius:999px;padding:2px 8px;margin-left:8px;vertical-align:middle;">Empfohlen</span>'
      : '';
    return '<div class="' + blockClass + '">'
      + '<div class="setup-code-label">' + label + chip + '</div>'
      + '<div class="setup-code-text">' + filled + '</div>'
      + '<div class="setup-code-actions">'
      +   '<button type="button" class="setup-code-copy js-copy-code" data-code="' + safeCode + '">'
      +     '<svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2" ry="2"/><path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 01-2-2V4a2 2 0 012-2h9a2 2 0 012 2v1"/></svg>'
      +     ' Kopieren'
      +   '</button>'
      +   '<a href="' + telHref + '" class="setup-code-dial">'
      +     '<svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M22 16.92v3a2 2 0 01-2.18 2 19.79 19.79 0 01-8.63-3.07A19.5 19.5 0 013.07 11 19.79 19.79 0 01.22 2.38 2 2 0 012.18 0h3a2 2 0 012 1.72c.13.96.36 1.9.7 2.81a2 2 0 01-.45 2.11L6.17 7.9a16 16 0 006.93 6.93l1.27-1.27a2 2 0 012.11-.45c.9.34 1.84.57 2.81.7A2 2 0 0122 16.92z"/></svg>'
      +     ' Anwählen'
      +   '</a>'
      + '</div>'
      + '</div>';
  }

  // Steps
  var stepsHtml = '<div style="margin-bottom:16px;">'
    + provider.steps.map(function(s, i) {
        return '<div class="setup-step">'
          + '<div class="setup-step-num">' + (i+1) + '</div>'
          + '<div><div class="setup-step-text">' + s.text + '</div>'
          + (s.sub ? '<div class="setup-step-sub">' + s.sub + '</div>' : '')
          + '</div></div>';
      }).join('')
    + '</div>';

  // Empfehlung-Intro vor den Codes
  var recommendedIntro = '<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink3);line-height:1.6;'
    + 'background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--surface-border);'
    + 'border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;margin-bottom:12px;">'
    + '<strong style="color:var(--ink2);">Empfehlung für Geschäftsnummern:</strong> '
    + 'Nutzen Sie <em>Bei Nichtannahme</em> — so bleiben Sie weiterhin selbst erreichbar '
    + 'und Voxera übernimmt nur wenn Sie nicht abnehmen.'
    + '</div>';

  // Codes (exclude off); miss = empfohlen
  var codesHtml = Object.entries(provider.codes)
    .filter(function(e) { return e[0] !== 'off'; })
    .map(function(e) { return makeCode(e[1].code, e[1].label, e[0] === 'miss'); })
    .join('');

  root.innerHTML = stepsHtml + recommendedIntro + codesHtml;

  // Deactivation
  if (deact) {
    var offCode = provider.codes.off;
    if (offCode) {
      deact.innerHTML = makeCode(offCode.code, offCode.label);
      document.getElementById('deactivation-card').style.display = 'block';
    }
  }

  // Wire up copy buttons via event delegation
  document.querySelectorAll('.js-copy-code').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.onclick = function() {
      var code = btn.getAttribute('data-code');
      if (code) copyCode(code);
    };
  });
}

// Test-Call State Machine
var _testCallPollTimer = null;
var _TEST_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS = 60000;
var _TEST_CALL_INTERVAL_MS = 3000;

function _doPollTestCall() {
  var elapsed = Date.now() - _testCallStartedAt;
  var remaining = Math.max(0, Math.round((_TEST_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS - elapsed) / 1000));

  if (elapsed >= _TEST_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS) {
    _stopTestCallPolling();
    _renderTestCallState('timeout');
    return;
  }

  loadData().then(function() {
    var currentCount = Array.isArray(allRecordsRaw) ? allRecordsRaw.length : 0;
    if (currentCount > _testCallStartCount) {
      _stopTestCallPolling();
      _renderTestCallState('success');
      updateSidebarStatus();
      return;
    }
    _renderTestCallState('polling', { remaining: remaining });
    _testCallPollTimer = setTimeout(_doPollTestCall, _TEST_CALL_INTERVAL_MS);
  }).catch(function(pollErr) {
    console.warn('[test-call] poll error:', pollErr);
    _testCallPollTimer = setTimeout(_doPollTestCall, _TEST_CALL_INTERVAL_MS);
  });
}
var _testCallStartCount = 0;
var _testCallStartedAt = 0;

function _renderTestCallState(state, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  // Suche in Shell zuerst, dann im Original
  var shell = document.getElementById('rufumleitung-shell');
  var btn = (shell && shell.querySelector('#test-call-btn')) || document.getElementById('test-call-btn');
  var result = (shell && shell.querySelector('#test-call-result')) || document.getElementById('test-call-result');
  if (!btn || !result) return;

  if (state === 'idle') {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Anruf prüfen';
    result.innerHTML = '';
  } else if (state === 'starting') {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = 'Wird gestartet…';
    result.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--slate);font-size:13px;">Testanruf wird vorbereitet…</span>';
  } else if (state === 'polling') {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = 'Anruf läuft…';
    var seconds = opts.remaining || 60;
    result.innerHTML = '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:14px;background:var(--blue-subtle);border:1px solid var(--blue-mid);border-radius:10px;">'
      + '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;background:#1A6FE8;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;">📞</div>'
      + '<div style="flex:1;font-size:13px;color:var(--blue);line-height:1.6;">'
      + '<strong style="display:block;margin-bottom:2px;color:var(--ink);">Voxera ruft jetzt Ihre Geschäftsnummer an</strong>'
      + 'Wenn die Weiterleitung aktiv ist, kommt der Anruf bei Voxera an. Bitte <strong>nicht</strong> abnehmen — lassen Sie es klingeln.'
      + '<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;color:var(--blue);">Wartezeit: noch ' + seconds + ' Sek.</div>'
      + '</div></div>';
  } else if (state === 'success') {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Erneut testen';
    result.innerHTML = '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:14px;background:rgba(5,150,105,.08);border:1px solid rgba(5,150,105,.25);border-radius:10px;">'
      + '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;background:#059669;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">✓</div>'
      + '<div style="flex:1;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink);line-height:1.6;">'
      + '<strong style="display:block;margin-bottom:2px;">Weiterleitung funktioniert</strong>'
      + 'Voxera hat den weitergeleiteten Anruf erfolgreich entgegengenommen. Ihr Assistent ist einsatzbereit.'
      + '</div></div>';
  } else if (state === 'timeout') {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Erneut testen';
    result.innerHTML = '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:14px;background:#FFFBEB;border:1px solid #FDE68A;border-radius:10px;">'
      + '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;background:#E8C547;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">!</div>'
      + '<div style="flex:1;font-size:13px;color:#92400E;line-height:1.6;">'
      + '<strong style="display:block;margin-bottom:2px;">Kein eingehender Anruf erkannt</strong>'
      + 'Die Weiterleitung scheint nicht aktiv zu sein. Bitte überprüfen Sie den Code für Ihren Anbieter und versuchen Sie es erneut.'
      + '</div></div>';
  } else if (state === 'error') {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Erneut versuchen';
    var msg = opts.message || 'Testanruf konnte nicht gestartet werden.';
    result.innerHTML = '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:14px;background:#FEF2F2;border:1px solid #FECACA;border-radius:10px;">'
      + '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;background:#DC2626;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">×</div>'
      + '<div style="flex:1;font-size:13px;color:#991B1B;line-height:1.6;">'
      + '<strong style="display:block;margin-bottom:2px;">Fehler beim Testanruf</strong>'
      + msg + '</div></div>';
  }
}

function _stopTestCallPolling() {
  if (_testCallPollTimer) {
    clearTimeout(_testCallPollTimer);
    _testCallPollTimer = null;
  }
}

async function checkTestCall() {
  // Reset previous state
  _stopTestCallPolling();
  _renderTestCallState('starting');

  // AbortController: bricht nach 12s ab (unter Netlify-10s-Limit mit Buffer)
  var abortCtrl = typeof AbortController !== 'undefined' ? new AbortController() : null;
  var abortTimer = abortCtrl
    ? setTimeout(function() { abortCtrl.abort(); }, 12000)
    : null;

  try {
    _testCallStartCount = Array.isArray(allRecordsRaw) ? allRecordsRaw.length : 0;
    _testCallStartedAt = Date.now();

    var json = await callDashboardFunction('activation-start-system-test-call', {},
      abortCtrl ? { signal: abortCtrl.signal } : {}
    );
    if (abortTimer) clearTimeout(abortTimer);

    if (!json || (json && json.error)) {
      throw new Error((json && json.error) || 'Testanruf konnte nicht gestartet werden.');
    }

    _renderTestCallState('polling', { remaining: 60 });
    _testCallPollTimer = setTimeout(_doPollTestCall, 3000);

  } catch(e) {
    if (abortTimer) clearTimeout(abortTimer);
    _stopTestCallPolling();
    // Differenzierte Fehlermeldung: Timeout vs. Netzwerk vs. Appfehler
    var userMsg;
    if (e.name === 'AbortError' || (e.message && e.message.toLowerCase().includes('abort'))) {
      userMsg = 'Die Verbindung zur Testanruf-Funktion hat zu lange gedauert (Timeout). '
        + 'Bitte versuchen Sie es in einem Moment erneut oder kontaktieren Sie den Support.';
    } else if (e.message && e.message.toLowerCase().includes('failed to fetch')) {
      userMsg = 'Verbindungsfehler — die Testanruf-Funktion war nicht erreichbar. '
        + 'Prüfen Sie Ihre Internetverbindung und versuchen Sie es erneut. '
        + 'Falls das Problem bleibt: <a href="mailto:support@voxera.ch" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;">support@voxera.ch</a>';
    } else {
      userMsg = (e.message || 'Unbekannter Fehler.')
        + ' — Falls das Problem bleibt: <a href="mailto:support@voxera.ch" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;">support@voxera.ch</a>';
    }
    _renderTestCallState('error', { message: userMsg });
  }
}

function renderEinrichtungTab() {
  // Update Voxera number
  var nr = (customerMeta && customerMeta.voxeraNumber) ? String(customerMeta.voxeraNumber) : '–';
  var el = document.getElementById('einrichtung-voxera-nr');
  if (el) el.textContent = nr;
  // Render status card + provider instructions
  renderEinrichtungStatus();
  renderProviderInstructions();
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   renderEinrichtungStatus()
   Befüllt die 4 Status-Felder oben auf der
   Erreichbarkeits-Seite.
   ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function renderEinrichtungStatus() {
  function setEl(id, html) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.innerHTML = html;
  }

  // ── 1. Weiterleitungsstatus ──────────────────────
  var fwdStatus = (customerMeta && customerMeta.forwardingStatus)
    ? String(customerMeta.forwardingStatus).trim().toLowerCase()
    : '';
  var fwdBadge;
  if (fwdStatus === 'active' || fwdStatus === 'aktiv') {
    fwdBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge active">'
      + '<svg width="8" height="8" viewBox="0 0 8 8" fill="currentColor"><circle cx="4" cy="4" r="4"/></svg>'
      + ' Aktiv</span>';
  } else if (fwdStatus === 'pending' || fwdStatus === 'einrichtung' || fwdStatus === 'setup') {
    fwdBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge amber">'
      + '<svg width="8" height="8" viewBox="0 0 8 8" fill="currentColor"><circle cx="4" cy="4" r="4"/></svg>'
      + ' Einrichtung läuft</span>';
  } else {
    fwdBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge neutral">Nicht aktiv</span>';
  }
  setEl('einrichtung-st-forwarding', fwdBadge);

  // ── 2. Voxera-Nummer ─────────────────────────────
  var nr = (customerMeta && customerMeta.voxeraNumber)
    ? String(customerMeta.voxeraNumber) : '–';
  setEl('einrichtung-st-number', nr);

  // ── 3. Vertragsstatus ────────────────────────────
  // Option A Frontend-Fallback:
  // customer-contract-state Function liest 'contracts'-Tabelle, Vertrag liegt aber in
  // 'subscriptions' → hasActiveContract bleibt false. Falls Plan bekannt → als Aktiv werten.
  var csRaw = (customerMeta && customerMeta.contractStatus) || '';
  var normalized = normalizeContractStatus(csRaw);
  var hasActivePlan = customerMeta
    && customerMeta.plan
    && customerMeta.plan !== 'Kein Plan'
    && customerMeta.plan !== '';
  var contractBadge;
  if (customerContractState && customerContractState.hasActiveContract) {
    // Sauberer Pfad: Function hat Vertrag gefunden
    contractBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge active">'
      + getContractStatusLabel(customerContractState.status || 'active') + '</span>';
  } else if (hasActivePlan) {
    // Fallback: Plan aus subscriptions-Tabelle geladen → Vertrag aktiv
    var planLabel = String(customerMeta.plan).charAt(0).toUpperCase()
      + String(customerMeta.plan).slice(1);
    contractBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge active">'
      + 'Aktiv &middot; ' + planLabel + '</span>';
  } else if (!normalized || normalized === 'unknown' || normalized === 'none') {
    contractBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge neutral">Kein Vertrag</span>';
  } else if (normalized === 'pending' || normalized === 'pending_review') {
    contractBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge amber">'
      + getContractStatusLabel(normalized) + '</span>';
  } else {
    contractBadge = '<span class="einrichtung-status-badge neutral">'
      + getContractStatusLabel(normalized) + '</span>';
  }
  setEl('einrichtung-st-contract', contractBadge);

  // ── 4. Letzter Anruf ─────────────────────────────
  // Achtung: allRecords ist offen-zuerst sortiert, NICHT nach Datum.
  // Deshalb: neustes created_at über alle Records suchen (O(n), klein).
  // Feld sitzt in record.fields.created_at (nicht direkt auf record).
  var lastCallHtml = '<span style="color:var(--ink3)">Noch kein Anruf</span>';
  try {
    if (allRecords && allRecords.length > 0) {
      var newestTs = 0;
      var newestRaw = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < allRecords.length; i++) {
        var f = (allRecords[i] && allRecords[i].fields) || {};
        var raw = f.created_at || '';
        if (!raw) continue;
        var d = parseCallTimestamp(raw);
        if (d && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) && d.getTime() > newestTs) {
          newestTs = d.getTime();
          newestRaw = raw;
        }
      }
      if (newestTs > 0) {
        var diffMs  = Date.now() - newestTs;
        var diffMin = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);
        var diffH   = Math.floor(diffMin / 60);
        var diffD   = Math.floor(diffH / 24);
        var relStr;
        if (diffMin < 2)       relStr = 'Gerade eben';
        else if (diffMin < 60) relStr = 'vor ' + diffMin + ' Min.';
        else if (diffH < 24)   relStr = 'vor ' + diffH + ' Std.';
        else if (diffD === 1)   relStr = 'Gestern';
        else if (diffD < 30)   relStr = 'vor ' + diffD + ' Tagen';
        else                   relStr = fmtDate(newestRaw) || 'vor langer Zeit';
        lastCallHtml = relStr;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  setEl('einrichtung-st-lastcall', lastCallHtml);
}

function showForgotPassword() {
  document.getElementById('login-card').innerHTML = `
    <img src="${BRAND_LOGO_PATH}" alt="Voxera" style="height:36px;width:auto;margin:0 auto 6px;display:block;"/>
    <div class="login-tag">Passwort zurücksetzen</div>
    <div class="login-sub">Geben Sie Ihre E-Mail-Adresse ein. Sie erhalten einen Link zum Zurücksetzen.</div>
    <div class="login-error" id="forgot-error"></div>
    <form class="login-form" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();doForgotPassword();">
      <div class="login-field">
        <label>E-Mail</label>
        <input type="email" id="forgot-email" placeholder="ihre@email.ch" autocomplete="email">
      </div>
      <button class="login-btn" id="forgot-btn" type="submit">Link senden</button>
    </form>
    <div class="login-links">
      <div class="login-note"><a href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="location.reload()">Zurück zum Login</a></div>
    </div>
  `;
  setTimeout(function(){
    var el = document.getElementById('forgot-email');
    if(el){ el.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') doForgotPassword(); }); el.focus(); }
  }, 100);
}

async function doForgotPassword() {
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return;
  var email = document.getElementById('forgot-email').value.trim();
  var btn = document.getElementById('forgot-btn');
  var errEl = document.getElementById('forgot-error');
  if (!email) { errEl.textContent='Bitte E-Mail eingeben.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) { errEl.textContent='Bitte eine gültige E-Mail eingeben.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='Wird gesendet...'; errEl.style.display='none';
  try {
    // redirectTo must be the clean dashboard URL without query params
    // This URL MUST be whitelisted in Supabase > Authentication > URL Configuration > Redirect URLs
    var redirectUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
    // Remove any trailing query string or hash
    if (redirectUrl.includes('?')) redirectUrl = redirectUrl.split('?')[0];
    var result = await sb.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, { redirectTo: redirectUrl });
    if (result.error) throw result.error;
    document.getElementById('login-card').innerHTML = '<div class=\"login-logo-wrap\"><div class=\"login-logo-ico\"><svg width=\"28\" height=\"28\" viewBox=\"0 0 48 48\" fill=\"none\"><path d=\"M24 6 C14 6,5 14,5 24\" stroke=\"#4B9FFF\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M24 6 C34 6,43 14,43 24\" stroke=\"#E8C547\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M5 24 C5 35,13 43,24 43 C35 43,43 35,43 24\" stroke=\"white\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M16 16 L24 29 L32 16\" stroke=\"white\" stroke-width=\"3.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\"/></svg></div><div class=\"login-logo\">VOXERA</div><div class=\"login-tag\">Kundenportal</div></div><div class=\"login-tag\">E-Mail gesendet</div><div class=\"login-sub\" style=\"color:var(--green);font-weight:600;\">Falls ein Konto mit dieser Adresse existiert, erhalten Sie in Kürze eine E-Mail.</div><div class=\"login-sub\" style=\"margin-top:8px;\">Prüfen Sie auch Ihren Spam-Ordner. Der Link ist 24 Stunden gültig.</div><div class=\"login-links\"><div class=\"login-note\"><a href=\"javascript:void(0)\" onclick=\"location.reload()\">Zurück zum Login</a></div></div>';
  } catch(e) {
    // User-friendly error messages
    var msg = (e.message || '').toLowerCase();
    if (msg.includes('rate limit') || msg.includes('too many')) {
      errEl.textContent = 'Zu viele Versuche. Bitte warten Sie einige Minuten.';
    } else if (msg.includes('not found') || msg.includes('no user')) {
      // Don't reveal if email exists - show success anyway
      document.getElementById('login-card').innerHTML = '<div class=\"login-logo-wrap\"><div class=\"login-logo-ico\"><svg width=\"28\" height=\"28\" viewBox=\"0 0 48 48\" fill=\"none\"><path d=\"M24 6 C14 6,5 14,5 24\" stroke=\"#4B9FFF\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M24 6 C34 6,43 14,43 24\" stroke=\"#E8C547\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M5 24 C5 35,13 43,24 43 C35 43,43 35,43 24\" stroke=\"white\" stroke-width=\"4\" stroke-linecap=\"round\"/><path d=\"M16 16 L24 29 L32 16\" stroke=\"white\" stroke-width=\"3.2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\"/></svg></div><div class=\"login-logo\">VOXERA</div><div class=\"login-tag\">Kundenportal</div></div><div class=\"login-tag\">E-Mail gesendet</div><div class=\"login-sub\" style=\"color:var(--green);font-weight:600;\">Falls ein Konto mit dieser Adresse existiert, erhalten Sie in Kürze eine E-Mail.</div><div class=\"login-links\"><div class=\"login-note\"><a href=\"javascript:void(0)\" onclick=\"location.reload()\">Zurück zum Login</a></div></div>';
      return;
    } else if (msg.includes('redirect') || msg.includes('url')) {
      errEl.textContent = 'Passwort-Reset ist aktuell nicht verfügbar. Bitte kontaktieren Sie den Support.';
    } else {
      errEl.textContent = 'Fehler beim Senden. Bitte versuchen Sie es erneut oder kontaktieren Sie den Support.';
    }
    errEl.style.display='block';
    btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='Link senden';
  }
}

async function getCurrentSessionUser() {
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return null;
  const { data: sessionData } = await sb.auth.getSession();
  if (sessionData && sessionData.session && sessionData.session.user) return sessionData.session.user;

  const { data: userData } = await sb.auth.getUser();
  return (userData && userData.user) || null;
}

async function doLogin() {
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return;
  const email = document.getElementById('login-email').value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value;
  const btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  const errEl = document.getElementById('login-error');

  if (!email || !password) {
    errEl.textContent = 'Bitte E-Mail und Passwort eingeben.';
    errEl.style.display = 'block';
    return;
  }

  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Anmelden...';
  errEl.style.display = 'none';

  try {
    isAuthTransition = true;
    isLogoutInProgress = false;
    setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
    const { data, error } = await sb.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) throw error;

    const user = (data && data.user) ? data.user : await getCurrentSessionUser();
    if (!user) throw new Error('Auth session missing');

    // Fresh login: always start on the dashboard tab, not a previously saved tab/detail state.
    forceDashboardOnNextRestore = true;
    window._vxForceDashboardAfterLogin = true;
    try {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('voxera_tab');
      sessionStorage.removeItem('voxera_last_non_setup_tab');
      vxSafeLocalStorageSet('voxera_tab', 'dashboard');
      vxSafeLocalStorageSet('currentTab', 'dashboard');
      vxSafeLocalStorageSet('activeTab', 'dashboard');
      history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
    } catch(e) {}
    if (typeof hideCallDetail === 'function') hideCallDetail();
    if (typeof closeDetail === 'function') closeDetail();
    if (typeof vxCloseDetailV2 === 'function') vxCloseDetailV2();
    window._vxActiveRecordId = null;
    window._vxCurrentCallId = null;
    window._vxCurrentTaskId = null;
    isLogoutInProgress = false;
    setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
    showApp(user);
    isAuthTransition = false;
  } catch(e) {
    isAuthTransition = false;
    const raw = (e && e.message) ? String(e.message) : '';
    const low = raw.toLowerCase();
    let msg = 'Login fehlgeschlagen.';
    if (low.includes('invalid') || low.includes('credentials')) msg = 'E-Mail oder Passwort falsch.';
    else if (low.includes('email not confirmed')) msg = 'E-Mail noch nicht bestätigt.';
    else if (low.includes('auth session missing')) msg = 'Anmeldung erfolgreich, aber Session fehlt. Bitte Seite neu laden und erneut anmelden.';
    else if (low.includes('unregistered') && low.includes('api key')) msg = 'Supabase API-Key ist ungültig (Unregistered API key). Bitte aktuellen Anon Key eintragen.';
    else if (raw) msg = raw;
    errEl.textContent = msg;
    errEl.style.display = 'block';
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Anmelden';
  }
}

function vxSetLoginFeedback(message, options) {
  var opts = options || {};
  var errEl = document.getElementById('login-error');
  if (errEl && message) {
    errEl.textContent = String(message);
    errEl.style.display = 'block';
  }
  var btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (btn) {
    btn.disabled = !!opts.disabled;
    btn.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    btn.textContent = opts.buttonText || (opts.disabled ? 'Anmelden...' : 'Anmelden');
  }
}

window.vxCustomerLoginSubmit = function vxCustomerLoginSubmit(event) {
  if (event && typeof event.preventDefault === 'function') event.preventDefault();
  var btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (btn && btn.disabled && btn.textContent !== 'Anmelden') return false;
  try {
    if (typeof doLogin !== 'function') {
      vxSetLoginFeedback('Login-Funktion konnte nicht geladen werden. Bitte Seite neu laden.', { disabled: false });
      console.error('[login] doLogin is not available');
      return false;
    }
    var result = doLogin();
    if (result && typeof result.catch === 'function') {
      result.catch(function(err) {
        console.error('[login] submit failed', err);
        vxSetLoginFeedback((err && err.message) ? err.message : 'Login fehlgeschlagen.', { disabled: false });
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[login] submit crashed', err);
    vxSetLoginFeedback((err && err.message) ? err.message : 'Login fehlgeschlagen.', { disabled: false });
  }
  return false;
};
window.doLogin = doLogin;

function vxBindLoginSubmitHandlers() {
  var form = document.querySelector('#login-screen form.login-form');
  var btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (form && !form.dataset.vxLoginBound) {
    form.dataset.vxLoginBound = '1';
    form.addEventListener('submit', window.vxCustomerLoginSubmit);
  }
  if (btn && !btn.dataset.vxLoginBound) {
    btn.dataset.vxLoginBound = '1';
    btn.addEventListener('click', window.vxCustomerLoginSubmit);
  }
}
if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', vxBindLoginSubmitHandlers);
} else {
  vxBindLoginSubmitHandlers();
}


async function doLogout() {
  isLogoutInProgress = true;
  isAuthTransition = true;
  showAppInProgress = false;
  currentUser = null; // sofort null setzen damit laufende loadData-Calls still abbrechen
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return;
  if (autoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(autoRefreshTimer);
  try {
    var signOutRes = await sb.auth.signOut({ scope: 'local' });
    if (signOutRes && signOutRes.error) throw signOutRes.error;
  } catch (signOutErr) {
    var fallbackNeeded = /scope|local|unsupported|invalid/i.test(String((signOutErr && signOutErr.message) || signOutErr || ''));
    if (!fallbackNeeded) throw signOutErr;
    var fallbackRes = await sb.auth.signOut();
    if (fallbackRes && fallbackRes.error) throw fallbackRes.error;
  }
  resetSetupSessionState();
  setupStateCustomerId = '';
  customerContext = { authUserId: null, usersCustomerId: null, customerId: null, customerRecord: null };
  showLogin();
  document.getElementById('login-password').value = '';
  // Re-enable login button in case it was disabled from a previous attempt
  var loginBtn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (loginBtn) { loginBtn.disabled = false; loginBtn.textContent = 'Anmelden'; }
  var loginErr = document.getElementById('login-error');
  if (loginErr) { loginErr.style.display = 'none'; loginErr.textContent = ''; }
  initNetworkCanvas();
  isLogoutInProgress = false;
  isAuthTransition = false;
}

function setLogoutButtonsLoading(isLoading) {
  var shouldLoad = !!isLoading;
  var buttons = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('button[onclick*="handleLogout"]'));
  buttons.forEach(function(btn) {
    if (!btn) return;
    if (!btn.dataset.logoutDefaultHtml) btn.dataset.logoutDefaultHtml = btn.innerHTML || 'Abmelden';
    if (shouldLoad) {
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
      btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-light ph-spinner-gap menu-icon" aria-hidden="true"></i>Melde ab...';
      return;
    }
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    btn.innerHTML = btn.dataset.logoutDefaultHtml || 'Abmelden';
  });
}

function showLogin() {
  isLogoutInProgress = false;
  isAuthTransition = false;
  setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
  var app = document.getElementById('app');
  if (app) app.style.display = 'none';
  var login = document.getElementById('login-screen');
  if (login) login.style.display = 'flex';
  var btn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (btn) {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    btn.textContent = 'Anmelden';
  }
}


let logoutInFlight = false;
async function handleLogout(triggerEl, beforeLogout) {
  if (logoutInFlight) return;
  logoutInFlight = true;
  var btn = triggerEl || null;
  var oldHtml = btn ? btn.innerHTML : '';
  var oldDisabled = btn ? !!btn.disabled : false;
  try {
    if (typeof beforeLogout === 'function') beforeLogout();
    setLogoutButtonsLoading(true);
    await doLogout();
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = (e && e.message) ? String(e.message) : 'Abmelden fehlgeschlagen';
    toast(msg);
    if (btn) {
      btn.disabled = oldDisabled;
      btn.innerHTML = oldHtml || 'Abmelden';
      btn.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    }
  } finally {
    setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
    logoutInFlight = false;
  }
}

function showSplash(onDone) {
  var splash = document.getElementById('splash-screen');
  var bar = document.getElementById('splash-bar');
  if (!splash) { onDone && onDone(); return; }
  // Show
  splash.classList.add('visible');
  // Animate bar
  requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      if (bar) bar.style.width = '100%';
    });
  });
  // After 1.4s fade out
  setTimeout(function() {
    splash.classList.add('fading');
    setTimeout(function() {
      splash.classList.remove('visible','fading');
      if (bar) bar.style.width = '0%';
      onDone && onDone();
    }, 300);
  }, 1400);
}

function delay(ms) {
  return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    setTimeout(resolve, Math.max(0, Number(ms) || 0));
  });
}

async function runInitialLoader(loadPromise, options) {
  var opts = options || {};
  var minVisibleMs = Number(opts.minVisibleMs || 1000);
  var splash = document.getElementById('splash-screen');
  var bar = document.getElementById('splash-bar');
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  if (splash) {
    splash.classList.add('visible');
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        if (bar) bar.style.width = '100%';
      });
    });
  }

  try {
    await loadPromise;
  } finally {
    var elapsed = Date.now() - startedAt;
    if (elapsed < minVisibleMs) {
      await delay(minVisibleMs - elapsed);
    }
    if (splash) {
      splash.classList.add('fading');
      await delay(300);
      splash.classList.remove('visible', 'fading');
    }
    if (bar) bar.style.width = '0%';
  }
}

function showApp(user) {
  if (showAppInProgress) return;
  showAppInProgress = true;
  isLogoutInProgress = false;
  isAuthTransition = false;
  setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
  if (autoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(autoRefreshTimer);
  teardownDashboardRealtime();
  currentUser = user || null;
  resetSetupSessionState();
  setupStateCustomerId = '';
  document.getElementById('login-screen').style.display = 'none';

  document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'none';

  // User display fallback from auth profile
  const meta = user.user_metadata || {};

  // Initial greeting, will be updated with customer_name after loadData
  const fallbackName = meta.full_name ? meta.full_name.split(' ')[0] : (user.email||'').split('@')[0];
  const greetingMain = document.getElementById('greeting-main');
  if (greetingMain) greetingMain.textContent = getTimeBasedGreeting() + ', ' + fallbackName;
  document.getElementById('sidebar-user').textContent = user.email;
  var initial = (user.email||'?')[0].toUpperCase();
  var avatarEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-avatar-cust');
  if(avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent = initial;
  var mobileAvatarEl = document.getElementById('mobile-header-avatar');
  if(mobileAvatarEl) mobileAvatarEl.textContent = initial;

  loadPlanConfigFromSupabase().catch(()=>{});
  updateSoundToggleUI(localStorage.getItem('voxera_sound_off') === '1');
  if (typeof updateBrowserNotifToggleUI === 'function') updateBrowserNotifToggleUI();
  var savedSound = localStorage.getItem('voxera_sound_type') || 'warm';
  selectSound(savedSound, true);

  var initialLoadSequence = loadData().then(function() {
    hasCompletedInitialDashboardLoad = true;
    var firstName = customerMeta.contactFirstName || (customerMeta.customerName ? customerMeta.customerName.split(' ')[0] : '') || fallbackName;
    if (greetingMain) {
      greetingMain.textContent = getTimeBasedGreeting() + ', ' + firstName;
      greetingMain.dataset.custom = '1';
    }
    restoreTab();
    setTimeout(function(){ if (typeof vxEnsureNoOrphanDetailView === 'function') vxEnsureNoOrphanDetailView('showApp:after-restoreTab'); }, 0);
    safeMountActivationV2({ reason: 'showApp', softGate: false });
    setTimeout(injectSortButtons, 100);
    tourCheckFirstLogin();
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (typeof vxPreloadAssistantVoices === 'function') {
        vxPreloadAssistantVoices().catch(function(){});
      }
    }, 300);
  }).catch(function(err) {
    showAppInProgress = false; // Guard freigeben bei Fehler
    console.error('[voxera-dashboard] initial load failed', err);
  });

  runInitialLoader(initialLoadSequence, { minVisibleMs: 1050 }).then(function() {
    document.getElementById('app').style.display = 'flex';
    window._vxRefreshIntervalMs = 12000;
    autoRefreshTimer = setInterval(safeLoadData, 12000);
    showAppInProgress = false;
  }).catch(function() {
    showAppInProgress = false; // Guard freigeben bei Fehler
  });
}

// Handle password recovery token in URL hash
async function handleRecoveryCode() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const code = params.get('code');
  const type = params.get('type');
  if (!code) return false;
  // Could be invite (type=invite) or recovery (type=recovery)
  // Exchange code for session
  try {
    const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
    if (!sb) return false;
    const { data, error } = await sb.auth.exchangeCodeForSession(code);
    if (error) {
      console.warn('[auth] code exchange failed:', error.message);
      return false;
    }
    // Clean URL
    history.replaceState({}, '', location.pathname);
    if (type === 'recovery' || (data.session && data.user)) {
      // For recovery: show password reset form
      if (type === 'recovery') {
        showPasswordResetForm(null, null);
        return true;
      }
      // For invite/signup: show app directly
      if (data.session && data.user) {
        showApp(data.user);
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('[auth] handleRecoveryCode error:', e);
  }
  return false;
}

function handleRecoveryHash() {
  const hash = location.hash;
  if (!hash) return false;
  const params = new URLSearchParams(hash.substring(1));
  if (params.get('type') === 'recovery' && params.get('access_token')) {
    showPasswordResetForm(params.get('access_token'), params.get('refresh_token'));
    history.replaceState({}, '', location.pathname + location.search);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// pendingResetTokens holds implicit-flow tokens; null when session already set (PKCE flow)
let pendingResetTokens = null;

function showPasswordResetForm(accessToken, refreshToken) {
  if (accessToken && refreshToken) {
    pendingResetTokens = { accessToken, refreshToken };
  } else {
    pendingResetTokens = null;
  }
  document.getElementById('login-screen').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('login-card').innerHTML = `
    <img src="${BRAND_LOGO_PATH}" alt="Voxera" style="height:36px;width:auto;margin:0 auto 6px;display:block;"/>
    <div class="login-tag">Neues Passwort</div>
    <div class="login-sub">Wählen Sie ein neues Passwort für Ihr Voxera Portal.</div>
    <div class="login-error" id="reset-error"></div>
    <form class="login-form" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();doReset();">
      <div class="login-field"><label>Neues Passwort</label><input type="password" id="new-pw" placeholder="Mindestens 8 Zeichen" autocomplete="new-password"></div>
      <div class="login-field"><label>Bestätigen</label><input type="password" id="confirm-pw" placeholder="Passwort wiederholen" autocomplete="new-password"></div>
      <button class="login-btn" id="reset-btn" type="submit">Passwort speichern</button>
    </form>
  `;
  // Wait for innerHTML to be inserted into the DOM before attaching listeners
  setTimeout(function(){
    var newPwInput = document.getElementById('new-pw');
    if (newPwInput) { newPwInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') document.getElementById('confirm-pw').focus(); }); newPwInput.focus(); }
    var confirmPwInput = document.getElementById('confirm-pw');
    if (confirmPwInput) confirmPwInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') doReset(); });
  }, 100);
}

async function doReset() {
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return;
  const pw = document.getElementById('new-pw').value;
  const pw2 = document.getElementById('confirm-pw').value;
  const btn = document.getElementById('reset-btn');
  const err = document.getElementById('reset-error');

  if (!pw || pw.length < 8) { err.textContent='Mind. 8 Zeichen.'; err.style.display='block'; return; }
  if (pw !== pw2) { err.textContent='Passwörter stimmen nicht überein.'; err.style.display='block'; return; }

  btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='Speichern...'; err.style.display='none';
  try {
    // Implicit flow: set session from URL hash tokens before updating password
    if (pendingResetTokens) {
      await sb.auth.setSession({ access_token: pendingResetTokens.accessToken, refresh_token: pendingResetTokens.refreshToken });
      pendingResetTokens = null;
    }
    const { error } = await sb.auth.updateUser({password: pw});
    if (error) throw error;
    document.getElementById('login-card').innerHTML = `
      <img src="${BRAND_LOGO_PATH}" alt="Voxera" style="height:36px;width:auto;margin:0 auto 6px;display:block;"/>
      <div class="login-sub" style="color:var(--green);font-weight:600;margin-top:20px;">Passwort erfolgreich gesetzt!</div>
      <div class="login-sub">Sie werden eingeloggt...</div>
    `;
    const { data } = await sb.auth.getSession();
    if (data.session) setTimeout(() => showApp(data.session.user), 1200);
  } catch(e) {
    err.textContent = e.message || 'Fehler.'; err.style.display='block';
    btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='Speichern';
  }
}

// Keyboard shortcuts
document.getElementById('login-password').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });
document.getElementById('login-email').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key==='Enter') document.getElementById('login-password').focus(); });

function initNetworkCanvas() {
  // Background handled by CSS gradient

function refreshLucideIcons() {
  /* phosphor: no init needed */
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  INIT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
const supabaseConfig = validateSupabaseConfig();
vxDebugInfo('[voxera-dashboard] build', APP_BUILD);
refreshLucideIcons();
if (!_sb || !_sb.auth) {
  showSystemUnavailableState();
} else if (!supabaseConfig.ok) {
  showConfigError(supabaseConfig.message);
} else if (handleRecoveryHash()) {
  // Showing password reset form (implicit flow / hash)
} else {
  // Check for PKCE code first, then fall back to session check
  const _urlCode = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('code');
  const _urlType = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('type');
  if (_urlCode) {
    handleRecoveryCode().then(function(handled) {
      if (!handled) {
        _sb.auth.getSession().then(function({ data: { session } }) {
          if (session) { showApp(session.user); }
          else { showLogin(); initNetworkCanvas(); }
        });
      }
    });
  } else {
    _sb.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      if (session) {
        showApp(session.user);
      } else {
        showLogin();
        initNetworkCanvas();
      }
    }).catch((err) => {
      const msg = String((err && err.message) || '').toLowerCase();
      if (msg.includes('unregistered') && msg.includes('api key')) {
        showConfigError('Supabase API-Key ungültig. Bitte aktuellen Anon Key eintragen.');
        return;
      }
      showLogin();
      initNetworkCanvas();
    });
  }
}

if (_sb && _sb.auth) {
  _sb.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
    if (event === 'SIGNED_OUT') {
      isAuthTransition = false;
      currentUser = null;
      if (autoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(autoRefreshTimer);
      teardownDashboardRealtime();
      showLogin();
    }
    if (event === 'SIGNED_IN' && session && session.user && document.getElementById('app').style.display === 'none') {
      isLogoutInProgress = false;
      isAuthTransition = false;
      setLogoutButtonsLoading(false);
      // Wenn gerade von activate.html weitergeleitet → App direkt zeigen, kein Recovery-Form
      showApp(session.user);
    }
    if (event === 'TOKEN_REFRESHED' && session) {
      vxDebugLog('Session refreshed');
    }
    // PASSWORD_RECOVERY: nur zeigen wenn User explizit einen Reset-Link geöffnet hat
    // NICHT nach Aktivierung (activate.html leitet mit sauberer Session weiter)
    if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') {
      // Prüfen ob wir von activate.html kommen – dann ignorieren
      const fromActivate = sessionStorage.getItem('voxera_just_activated');
      if (fromActivate) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('voxera_just_activated');
        // Session ist bereits authentifiziert – App zeigen
        if (session && session.user) showApp(session.user);
        return;
      }
      showPasswordResetForm(null, null);
    }
  });
}

// ─── Avatar / Profile Photo ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderProfilePhoto(url, email) {
  var circle = document.getElementById('profile-photo-circle');
  var initials = document.getElementById('profile-photo-initials');
  var sidebarAvatar = document.getElementById('sidebar-avatar-cust');
  var mobileAvatar = document.getElementById('mobile-header-avatar');
  var initial = (email || customerMeta.customerEmail || '?')[0].toUpperCase();

  if (url) {
    // Show photo
    if (initials) initials.style.display = 'none';
    if (circle) {
      var img = circle.querySelector('img') || document.createElement('img');
      img.src = url;
      img.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%;';
      if (!circle.querySelector('img')) circle.appendChild(img);
    }
    // Small avatars: show photo too
    if (sidebarAvatar) { sidebarAvatar.innerHTML = '<img src="'+url+'" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:8px;">'; }
    if (mobileAvatar) { mobileAvatar.innerHTML = '<img src="'+url+'" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:8px;">'; }
  } else {
    if (initials) { initials.style.display = ''; initials.textContent = initial; }
    if (sidebarAvatar) { sidebarAvatar.textContent = initial; sidebarAvatar.innerHTML = initial; }
    if (mobileAvatar) { mobileAvatar.textContent = initial; }
  }
}

async function uploadAvatar(input) {
  var file = input.files && input.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  var status = document.getElementById('avatar-upload-status');
  if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    if (status) status.textContent = 'Datei zu gross (max. 2 MB).';
    return;
  }
  if (status) status.textContent = 'Wird hochgeladen...';

  var sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) { if (status) status.textContent = 'Fehler: Nicht eingeloggt.'; return; }

  var customerId = customerMeta.customerId;
  if (!customerId) { if (status) status.textContent = 'Fehler: Kunden-ID fehlt.'; return; }

  var ext = file.name.split('.').pop() || 'jpg';
  var path = customerId + '/logo.' + ext;

  try {
    // Upload to Supabase Storage
    var { error: upErr } = await sb.storage.from('avatars').upload(path, file, {
      upsert: true, contentType: file.type
    });
    if (upErr) throw upErr;

    // Get public URL
    var { data: urlData } = sb.storage.from('avatars').getPublicUrl(path);
    var publicUrl = urlData && urlData.publicUrl;
    if (!publicUrl) throw new Error('URL konnte nicht ermittelt werden.');

    // Save to customer record via function
    var res = await callDashboardFunction('customer-update-settings', { avatar_url: publicUrl });
    if (!res || !res.success) throw new Error((res && res.error) || 'Speichern fehlgeschlagen.');

    customerMeta.avatarUrl = publicUrl;
    renderProfilePhoto(publicUrl);
    if (status) status.textContent = 'Gespeichert.';
    setTimeout(() => { if (status) status.textContent = ''; }, 3000);
  } catch (e) {
    if (status) status.textContent = 'Fehler: ' + (e.message || e);
  }
  input.value = '';
}


// ─── Accordion Call Rows ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function toggleCallRow(event, row) {
  if (event.target.closest('button, a')) return;
  var isExpanded = row.classList.contains('expanded');
  // Nur öffnen — nie automatisch schliessen
  if (isExpanded) {
    row.classList.remove('expanded');
  } else {
    row.classList.add('expanded');
    if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
      setTimeout(function() {
        row.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest'});
      }, 50);
    }
  }
}

        --white ist laut Stage 6 G0 absichtlich NICHT remappt → bleibt
        #FFFFFF. JS (selectNotifCard Z.10271, selectSound Z.10303) setzt
        denselben Wert dynamisch nach. Inline-Spec (1,0,0,0) → !important.
        Aktive Optionen (var(--blue-light)) funktionieren bereits.

   W5 — Hilfe Tour-Banner (Z.4932)
        Inline-Style mit hardcoded #F0F7FF / #BFDBFE / #1E3A5F. Werte
        exklusiv für diese Stelle (verifiziert). Kein Klassen-/ID-Anchor →
        Attribut-Selektor mit !important.
        Nicht verwechseln mit #tour-card in Stage 6 G7 — das ist das
        Tooltip-Popup, nicht der Hilfe-Seiten-Banner.

   W6 — dash-glass Container-Header + Card-Trennlinien
        W6.1 .dash-glass-hd background:#FAFCFF (Z.2110, kein !important).
             Stage 2 fixt nur border-bottom-color, nicht background.
        W6.2 .dash-glass .call-row border-bottom:1px solid #EDF2FA
             !important (Z.2135). Stage 3 hatte richtige Farbe ohne
             !important → verloren. Mit !important nachgezogen +
             0.5px shorthand (Stage-3-Designintention).
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* — W4: Notif/Sound-Picker inaktive Optionen — */
/* Beide Substring-Varianten: HTML-inline schreibt ohne Space,
   JS-set normalisiert je nach Browser ggf. mit Space nach Doppelpunkt. */

<script>
var TOUR_STORAGE_KEY = 'voxera_tour_v2_done';
var _tourStep = 0;
var _tourActive = false;

var TOUR_STEPS = [];

function tourBuildSteps() {
  var assistantName = String(customerMeta?.assistantName || 'Lara').trim() || 'Lara';
  var displayName = String(customerMeta?.displayName || customerMeta?.contactFirstName || customerMeta?.customerName || 'Willkommen').trim();
  var greeting = String(customerMeta?.greeting || '').trim() || vxBuildAutoGreeting();
  var quoteHtml = '<div style="background:#F8FAFC;border-left:3px solid #1A6FE8;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:0 8px 8px 0;font-style:italic;color:#13284D;margin-top:8px;">„' + greeting.replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;') + '“</div>';
  return [
    { target:null, position:'center', iconBg:'#0D1F3C', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-sparkle" style="color:#fff;"></i>', title:'Willkommen bei Voxera, ' + displayName + '.', text:'Ihr Assistent ' + assistantName + ' ist eingerichtet und bereit für Ihren ersten Anruf. Diese Tour dauert 60 Sekunden.' },
    { tab:'assistent', target:'nav-assistent', mobileTarget:'nav-assistent', iconBg:'#EEF4FF', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-microphone" style="color:#1A6FE8;"></i>', title: assistantName + ' spricht in Ihrem Namen.', text:'Ton, Anrede und Stimme sind bereits konfiguriert. Sie können jederzeit anpassen.' },
    { tab:'assistent', target:'assistent-greeting-visible', mobileTarget:'assistent-greeting-visible', iconBg:'#EEF4FF', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-chat-circle" style="color:#1A6FE8;"></i>', title:'So meldet sich ' + assistantName + ' bei Ihren Kunden.', text:'', htmlText: quoteHtml },
    { tab:'assistent', target:'vxi-profil-desc', mobileTarget:'vxi-profil-desc', iconBg:'#EEF4FF', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-buildings" style="color:#1A6FE8;"></i>', title: assistantName + ' kennt Ihr Unternehmen.', text:'Geschäftsprofil und Leistungen sind hinterlegt. Je mehr Details, desto besser die Antworten.' },
    { tab:'anrufe', target:'nav-anrufe', mobileTarget:'nav-anrufe', iconBg:'#EEF4FF', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-phone" style="color:#1A6FE8;"></i>', title:'Jeder Anruf — vollständig dokumentiert.', text:'KI-Zusammenfassung, Kategorie und Lead-Qualität für jeden eingehenden Anruf. Nachfassaktionen direkt setzen.' },
    { tab:'heute', target:'nav-heute', mobileTarget:'nav-heute', iconBg:'#EEF4FF', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-monitor" style="color:#1A6FE8;"></i>', title:'Ihr Tages-Cockpit.', text:'Offene Anfragen, überfällige Rückrufe und die wichtigsten Anrufe — immer auf einen Blick.' },
    { target:null, position:'center', iconBg:'#0D1F3C', icon:'<i class="ph-bold ph-check" style="color:#fff;"></i>', title:'Sie sind startklar.', text:'Letzte Aufgabe: Rufumleitung einrichten, damit Voxera Ihre Anrufe entgegennehmen kann.', isLast:true, ctaText:'Rufumleitung einrichten', action:'forwarding' }
  ];
}

function tourGetTargetRect(targetId, mobileTargetId) {
  var isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;
  var id = (isMobile && mobileTargetId) ? mobileTargetId : targetId;
  if (!id) return null;
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) return null;
  var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (r.width === 0 && r.height === 0) return null;
  return { x: r.left - 10, y: r.top - 8, w: r.width + 20, h: r.height + 16 };
}

function tourDrawOverlay(rect) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('tour-canvas');
  var W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight;
  canvas.width = W; canvas.height = H;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(13,31,60,0.72)';
  if (!rect) {
    ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  } else {
    var r = 10;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.rect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx.moveTo(rect.x + r, rect.y);
    ctx.lineTo(rect.x + rect.w - r, rect.y);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(rect.x + rect.w, rect.y, rect.x + rect.w, rect.y + r);
    ctx.lineTo(rect.x + rect.w, rect.y + rect.h - r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(rect.x + rect.w, rect.y + rect.h, rect.x + rect.w - r, rect.y + rect.h);
    ctx.lineTo(rect.x + r, rect.y + rect.h);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(rect.x, rect.y + rect.h, rect.x, rect.y + rect.h - r);
    ctx.lineTo(rect.x, rect.y + r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(rect.x, rect.y, rect.x + r, rect.y);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill('evenodd');
    // Glow ring
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(26,111,232,0.6)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.strokeRect(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h);
  }
}

function tourPositionCard(rect, isMobile) {
  var card = document.getElementById('tour-card');
  if (!card) return;
  var W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight;
  isMobile = isMobile || W < 768;
  var cardW = isMobile ? Math.min(W - 32, 320) : 300;
  card.style.width = cardW + 'px';

  if (!rect) {
    card.style.left = Math.max(16, (W - cardW) / 2) + 'px';
    card.style.top = Math.max(16, (H - 360) / 2) + 'px';
    return;
  }

  var margin = 16;
  var cardH = card.offsetHeight || 300;
  var left, top;

  if (isMobile) {
    left = (W - cardW) / 2;
    if (rect.y > H * 0.65) {
      top = Math.max(80, rect.y - cardH - margin - 20);
    } else {
      top = Math.min(rect.y + rect.h + margin, H - cardH - 80);
      if (top + cardH > H - 80) top = Math.max(80, rect.y - cardH - margin);
    }
  } else {
    if (W - rect.x - rect.w >= cardW + margin) {
      left = rect.x + rect.w + margin;
      top = Math.max(20, Math.min(rect.y, H - cardH - 20));
    } else if (rect.x >= cardW + margin) {
      left = rect.x - cardW - margin;
      top = Math.max(20, Math.min(rect.y, H - cardH - 20));
    } else {
      left = (W - cardW) / 2;
      top = rect.y + rect.h + margin;
      if (top + cardH > H - 20) top = Math.max(20, rect.y - cardH - margin);
    }
  }

  card.style.left = Math.max(16, Math.min(left, W - cardW - 16)) + 'px';
  card.style.top = Math.max(16, Math.min(top, H - cardH - 16)) + 'px';
}

function tourNavigate(step) {
  if (!step.tab) return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-' + step.tab);
    showTab(step.tab, navEl, { historyMode: 'none' });
    setTimeout(resolve, 400);
  });
}

function tourRender() {
  var step = TOUR_STEPS[_tourStep];
  var isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;
  var rect = tourGetTargetRect(step.target, step.mobileTarget);

  tourDrawOverlay(rect);
  tourPositionCard(rect, isMobile);

  var iconEl = document.getElementById('tour-icon');
  if (iconEl) { iconEl.innerHTML = step.icon; iconEl.style.background = step.iconBg || '#EEF4FF'; }
  if (typeof refreshLucideIcons === 'function') refreshLucideIcons();
  document.getElementById('tour-title').textContent = step.title;
  var textEl = document.getElementById('tour-text');
  if (textEl) {
    if (step.htmlText) textEl.innerHTML = step.htmlText;
    else textEl.textContent = step.text || '';
  }
  var progressEl = document.getElementById('tour-progress');
  if (progressEl) progressEl.textContent = (_tourStep + 1) + ' / ' + TOUR_STEPS.length;
  var barEl = document.getElementById('tour-progress-bar');
  if (barEl) barEl.style.width = Math.round(((_tourStep + 1) / TOUR_STEPS.length) * 100) + '%';
  var backBtn = document.getElementById('tour-back-btn');
  var nextBtn = document.getElementById('tour-next-btn');
  if (backBtn) backBtn.style.display = _tourStep > 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (nextBtn) { nextBtn.textContent = step.ctaText || (step.isLast ? 'Fertig' : 'Weiter'); nextBtn.style.background = step.isLast ? '#1A6FE8' : '#0D1F3C'; }
}

function tourGoToStep(idx) {
  _tourStep = idx;
  var step = TOUR_STEPS[idx];
  var card = document.getElementById('tour-card');
  if (card) { card.style.animation = 'none'; requestAnimationFrame(function() { card.style.animation = 'tourCardIn .25s cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1)'; }); }
  if (step.tab) {
    tourNavigate(step).then(function() { tourRender(); });
  } else {
    tourRender();
  }
}

function tourStart() {
  TOUR_STEPS = tourBuildSteps();
  _tourStep = 0;
  _tourActive = true;
  // Flag sofort beim Start setzen — Abbrechen = auch "gesehen", nie mehr auto-starten
  try { vxSafeLocalStorageSet(TOUR_STORAGE_KEY, '1'); } catch(_e) {}
  var overlay = document.getElementById('tour-overlay');
  overlay.style.display = 'block';
  overlay.style.pointerEvents = 'all';
  tourGoToStep(0);
}

function tourNext() {
  var step = TOUR_STEPS[_tourStep] || {};
  if (step.action === 'forwarding') {
    tourDone();
    setTimeout(function(){ try { openSetupWizardFromCta(null, 's1'); } catch(_e) {} }, 120);
    return;
  }
  if (_tourStep >= TOUR_STEPS.length - 1) { tourDone(); return; }
  tourGoToStep(_tourStep + 1);
}

function tourBack() {
  if (_tourStep <= 0) return;
  tourGoToStep(_tourStep - 1);
}

function tourSkip() { tourDone(); }

function tourDone() {
  _tourActive = false;
  var overlay = document.getElementById('tour-overlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
  try { vxSafeLocalStorageSet(TOUR_STORAGE_KEY, '1'); } catch(_e) {}
  var navEl = document.querySelector('.nav-item');
  showTab('dashboard', navEl, { historyMode: 'none' });
}

function tourCheckFirstLogin() {
  try { if (!localStorage.getItem(TOUR_STORAGE_KEY)) setTimeout(tourStart, 1400); } catch(_e) {}
}

window.addEventListener('resize', function() { if (_tourActive) tourRender(); });
</script>

<script>
/* Slim stub: vxOpenDetailV2 redirects to new routing; vxDv2SaveNote kept for fullscreen note-save */
(function() {
  // vxDv2SaveNote: saves note from fullscreen detail view into Supabase
  window.vxDv2SaveNote = function() {
    var recId = String(window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || '').trim();
    if (!recId) return;
    var field = document.getElementById('vx-dv2-note');
    var btn = document.getElementById('vx-dv2-save-btn');
    if (!field) return;
    var noteText = field.value.trim();
    if (btn) { btn.textContent = 'Speichert…'; btn.disabled = true; }
    var resetBtn = function(label, bg) {
      if (!btn) return;
      btn.textContent = label || 'Speichern';
      btn.style.background = bg || '#0D1F3C';
      btn.disabled = false;
    };
    var sb = window.getSupabaseAuthClient ? getSupabaseAuthClient() : window._sb;
    if (!sb) { resetBtn('Speichern'); if (window.toast) toast('Verbindung fehlt.'); return; }
    sb.from('calls').update({ notes_customer_voxera: noteText, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', recId)
      .then(function() {
        var rec = (window.allRecords||[]).find(function(r){ return r.id === recId; });
        if (rec && rec.fields) rec.fields.notes_customer_voxera = noteText;
        resetBtn('Gespeichert ✓', '#059669');
        setTimeout(function(){ resetBtn('Speichern', '#0D1F3C'); }, 2000);
        if (window.renderDashPriorityList) renderDashPriorityList();
      }).catch(function() {
        resetBtn('Fehler', '#DC2626');
        if (window.toast) toast('Notiz konnte nicht gespeichert werden.');
      });
  };

  // vxOpenDetailV2: redirect to new fullscreen routing
  window.vxOpenDetailV2 = function(recordId) {
    if (typeof window.showCallDetail === 'function') window.showCallDetail(recordId);
  };

  // vxCloseDetailV2: no-op stub (old bottom-sheet removed)
  window.vxCloseDetailV2 = function() {
    if (typeof window.vxCloseDetailV2Fullscreen === 'function') window.vxCloseDetailV2Fullscreen();
  };
})();
</script>
<script>
// ── Nachfassen Reminder Logic ─────────────────────
(function() {
  var _reminderMin = 15;
  var _reminderTimer = null;

  function vxInitReminderUI() {
    var cb = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-on');
    var opts = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-opts');
    if (!cb || !opts) return;
    cb.addEventListener('change', function() {
      opts.style.display = this.checked ? 'block' : 'none';
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.followup-reminder-opt').forEach(function(btn) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        _reminderMin = parseInt(this.dataset.min);
        document.querySelectorAll('.followup-reminder-opt').forEach(function(b) {
          b.style.background = '#fff'; b.style.borderColor = '#BFDBFE'; b.style.color = '#1A6FE8';
        });
        this.style.background = '#1A6FE8'; this.style.borderColor = '#1A6FE8'; this.style.color = '#fff';
        var sel = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-selected');
        if (sel) sel.textContent = '✓ Erinnerung ' + _reminderMin + ' Minuten vorher';
      });
    });
  }

  function vxScheduleReminder(name, followUpAt, minutesBefore) {
    if (_reminderTimer) clearTimeout(_reminderTimer);
    if (!followUpAt) return;
    var fuStr = followUpAt + (followUpAt.includes('Z') || followUpAt.includes('+') ? '' : 'Z');
    var fuTime = new Date(fuStr).getTime();
    var delay = fuTime - (minutesBefore * 60 * 1000) - Date.now();
    if (delay < 0 || delay > 86400000) return;
    _reminderTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      if ('Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
        new Notification('Voxera — Rückruf in ' + minutesBefore + ' Min', {
          body: name ? 'Rückruf bei ' + name : 'Rückruf steht an',
          icon: '/favicon.ico'
        });
      } else if (window.showToast) {
        showToast('⏰ Erinnerung: Rückruf' + (name ? ' bei ' + name : '') + ' steht an!', 'info', 8000);
      }
    }, delay);
  }

  function vxRequestNotificationPermission(cb) {
    if (!('Notification' in window)) { if(cb) cb(false); return; }
    if (Notification.permission === 'granted') { if(cb) cb(true); return; }
    if (Notification.permission === 'denied') { if(cb) cb(false); return; }
    Notification.requestPermission().then(function(p) { if(cb) cb(p === 'granted'); });
  }

  function vxGetReminderSettings() {
    var cb = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-on');
    return { active: cb && cb.checked, minutesBefore: _reminderMin };
  }

  function vxResetReminderUI() {
    var cb = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-on');
    var opts = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-opts');
    if (cb) cb.checked = false;
    if (opts) opts.style.display = 'none';
    _reminderMin = 15;
    document.querySelectorAll('.followup-reminder-opt').forEach(function(b) {
      b.style.background = '#fff'; b.style.borderColor = '#BFDBFE'; b.style.color = '#1A6FE8';
    });
    var sel = document.getElementById('followup-reminder-selected');
    if (sel) sel.textContent = '';
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', vxInitReminderUI);
  } else {
    vxInitReminderUI();
  }

  window.vxScheduleReminder = vxScheduleReminder;
  window.vxGetReminderSettings = vxGetReminderSettings;
  window.vxResetReminderUI = vxResetReminderUI;
  window.vxRequestNotificationPermission = vxRequestNotificationPermission;
})();
</script>

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PATCH v2.23 — GLOBALER KONSISTENZ-PASS
   Ziel: Design-Token-Enforcement ohne 400 Einzelstellen zu ändern.
   Strategie: Hohe Spezifität am Ende der Cascade schlägt hardcoded Werte.

   1. FARB-TOKENS: #1A6FE8 → var(--blue), #0D1F3C → var(--vx-night)
   2. BORDER-RADIUS: Normalisierung auf 4 Stufen (sm/md/lg/full)
   3. MODAL-SYSTEM: .btn Basis für alle Modal-Buttons
   4. SETTINGS/ASSISTENT: Harmonisierung mit Hauptdesign
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* ── 1. FARB-TOKEN ENFORCEMENT ─────────────────────────────── */
/* Primärfarbe: hardcodierte #1A6FE8 als TEXT-FARBE auf Token setzen.
   ACHTUNG: Nur matchen wenn es sich um color (nicht background) handelt.
   Selektoren mit background:#1A6FE8 (Buttons, Cards) bleiben unberührt —
   deren color:#fff darf NICHT überschrieben werden. */
a[style*="color:#1A6FE8"],
span[style*="color:#1A6FE8"],
div[style*="color:#1A6FE8"] { color: var(--blue) !important; }

/* Buttons mit blauem Hintergrund: Text IMMER weiss — niemals überschreiben */

<script>
// ── Split-View Resizer ────────────────────────────────────
(function(){
  var resizer = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-resizer');
  var leftPanel = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-left');
  if (!resizer || !leftPanel) return;
  
  var startX = 0, startW = 0, dragging = false;
  var DEFAULT_W = 440, MIN_W = 390, MAX_RATIO = 0.6;
  function clampWidth(candidate) {
    var container = leftPanel.parentElement;
    var maxW = Math.floor((container ? container.offsetWidth : window.innerWidth || 0) * MAX_RATIO);
    if (!isFinite(maxW) || maxW <= MIN_W) maxW = MIN_W;
    return Math.min(Math.max(candidate, MIN_W), maxW);
  }
  function applyWidth(nextW) {
    var clamped = clampWidth(nextW);
    leftPanel.style.setProperty('--vx-split-left-w', clamped + 'px');
    leftPanel.style.setProperty('width', clamped + 'px', 'important');
    leftPanel.style.setProperty('flex-basis', clamped + 'px', 'important');
    return clamped;
  }

  function onMouseMove(e) {
    if (!dragging) return;
    var delta = e.clientX - startX;
    var applied = applyWidth(startW + delta);
    try { vxSafeLocalStorageSet('vx_split_w', applied); } catch(e) {}
  }

  function onMouseUp() {
    if (!dragging) return;
    dragging = false;
    resizer.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor = '';
    document.body.style.userSelect = '';
    document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
    document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
  }

  resizer.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    dragging = true;
    startX = e.clientX;
    startW = leftPanel.offsetWidth;
    resizer.classList.add('is-dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
  });

  // Restore saved width (legacy values are clamped).
  try {
    var rawSaved = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('vx_split_w'), 10);
    var initial = Number.isFinite(rawSaved) ? rawSaved : DEFAULT_W;
    var applied = applyWidth(initial);
    vxSafeLocalStorageSet('vx_split_w', applied);
  } catch(e) {}
})();

(function(){
  var resizer = document.getElementById('archiv-split-resizer');
  var leftPanel = document.getElementById('archiv-split-left');
  if (!resizer || !leftPanel) return;
  var startX = 0, startW = 0, dragging = false;
  var DEFAULT_W = 440, MIN_W = 300, MAX_RATIO = 0.6;
  function clampW(c) {
    var maxW = Math.floor((leftPanel.parentElement ? leftPanel.parentElement.offsetWidth : window.innerWidth) * MAX_RATIO);
    if (!isFinite(maxW) || maxW <= MIN_W) maxW = MIN_W;
    return Math.min(Math.max(c, MIN_W), maxW);
  }
  function applyW(w) {
    var clamped = clampW(w);
    leftPanel.style.setProperty('width', clamped + 'px', 'important');
    leftPanel.style.setProperty('flex-basis', clamped + 'px', 'important');
    return clamped;
  }
  function onMM(e) { if (dragging) applyW(startW + e.clientX - startX); }
  function onMU() {
    if (!dragging) return;
    dragging = false;
    resizer.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor = '';
    document.body.style.userSelect = '';
    document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMM);
    document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMU);
  }
  resizer.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault(); dragging = true; startX = e.clientX; startW = leftPanel.offsetWidth;
    resizer.classList.add('is-dragging');
    document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMM);
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMU);
  });
  applyW(DEFAULT_W);
})();
</script>

<script>
/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   VOXERA — Detail-Header Menu Helpers · 2026-05-17
   Public functions used by the rebuilt detail header:
     vxdToggleMenu(e)  — toggles the #vxd-menu popover
     vxdCloseMenu()    — closes the popover (used by each menu item)
   The body click listener auto-closes the menu on outside click.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
(function() {
  function vxdMenuEl() { return document.getElementById('vxd-menu'); }
  function vxdTriggerEl() { return document.getElementById('vxd-menu-trigger'); }

  function vxdToggleMenu(e) {
    if (e && typeof e.preventDefault === 'function') e.preventDefault();
    var m = vxdMenuEl();
    var t = vxdTriggerEl();
    if (!m) return;
    var willOpen = m.hasAttribute('hidden');
    if (willOpen) {
      m.removeAttribute('hidden');
      if (t) t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    } else {
      m.setAttribute('hidden', '');
      if (t) t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }
  }

  function vxdCloseMenu() {
    var m = vxdMenuEl();
    var t = vxdTriggerEl();
    if (m) m.setAttribute('hidden', '');
    if (t) t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }

  // Outside click → close
  document.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
    var m = vxdMenuEl();
    if (!m || m.hasAttribute('hidden')) return;
    var t = vxdTriggerEl();
    if (m.contains(ev.target)) return;
    if (t && t.contains(ev.target)) return;
    vxdCloseMenu();
  }, true);

  // ESC → close
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev) {
    if (ev.key === 'Escape' || ev.keyCode === 27) {
      var m = vxdMenuEl();
      if (m && !m.hasAttribute('hidden')) vxdCloseMenu();
    }
  });

  window.vxdToggleMenu = vxdToggleMenu;
  window.vxdCloseMenu = vxdCloseMenu;
})();
</script>

<script>
/* VOXERA — Mobile detail option B guard. Keeps the bottom bar in sync
   without touching desktop split/focus logic. */
(function(){
  function isMobile(){ return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width:768px)').matches; }
  function syncMobileDetailBar(){
    if(!isMobile()) return;
    var page=document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    var bar=document.getElementById('vx-cd-bottom-bar');
    if(!page||!bar) return;
    var open=page.classList.contains('is-open') || page.style.display==='flex' || page.style.display==='block';
    bar.style.display=open?'grid':'none';
    var phoneEl=document.getElementById('call-detail-subtitle');
    var callBtn=document.getElementById('vx-cd-bb-call');
    var phone=((phoneEl && (phoneEl.getAttribute('aria-label')||phoneEl.textContent||'')) || '').trim();
    if(callBtn){
      var hasPhone=!!phone && phone.indexOf('Keine Telefonnummer')<0;
      callBtn.disabled=!hasPhone;
      callBtn.classList.toggle('is-disabled',!hasPhone);
      callBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled',hasPhone?'false':'true');
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',function(){ setTimeout(syncMobileDetailBar,60); },true);
  window.addEventListener('resize',syncMobileDetailBar,{passive:true});
  var oldRender=window.renderCallDetailPage;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function' && !oldRender._vxMobileOptionBWrapped){
    var wrapped=function(){
      var res=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(syncMobileDetailBar,30);
      return res;
    };
    wrapped._vxMobileOptionBWrapped=true;
    window.renderCallDetailPage=wrapped;
  }
  window.vxSyncMobileDetailBar=syncMobileDetailBar;
})();
</script>

<script>
(function vxAssertGlobalDomPlacement(){
  var globalSelectors = [
    '.mobile-nav',
    '#call-detail-page',
    '#vx-detail-v2',
    '#vx-notif-panel',
    '#vx-notif-backdrop',
    '#vx-toast-container'
  ];
  function run(){
    globalSelectors.forEach(function(sel){
      var el = document.querySelector(sel);
      if (!el) return;
      var parentTab = el.closest('.tab-page');
      if (parentTab) {
        console.warn('[Voxera DOM Guard] Global element is inside tab-page:', sel, parentTab.id);
      }
    });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', run);
  else run();
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-request-row-and-detail-header-stability-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';

  function qsa(sel, root){ return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }
  function getRowId(el){
    if(!el) return '';
    var card = el.closest && el.closest('.dpr-card');
    var row = el.closest && el.closest('.vx-heute-row,.vx-anf-row');
    return String((card && (card.dataset.id || card.dataset.recordId)) || (row && (row.dataset.id || row.dataset.recordId)) || '');
  }
  function setActiveRequestRow(id){
    if(!id) return;
    qsa('#dash-priority-list .dpr-card.sp-active,#anrufe-list .dpr-card.sp-active,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card.sp-active').forEach(function(el){
      if(String(el.dataset.id || el.dataset.recordId || '') !== String(id)) el.classList.remove('sp-active');
    });
    qsa('#dash-priority-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card').forEach(function(card){
      var cid = String(card.dataset.id || card.dataset.recordId || '');
      if(cid === String(id)) card.classList.add('sp-active');
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var row = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#dash-priority-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-list .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row');
    if(!row) return;
    var id = getRowId(row);
    if(id) setActiveRequestRow(id);
  }, true);

  var oldShow = window.showCallDetail;
  if(typeof oldShow === 'function' && !oldShow._vxStableActiveWrapped){
    var wrapped = function(callId){
      if(callId) setActiveRequestRow(callId);
      return oldShow.apply(this, arguments);
    };
    wrapped._vxStableActiveWrapped = true;
    window.showCallDetail = wrapped;
  }
  window.vxSetActiveRequestRow = setActiveRequestRow;

  function syncDetailScrolledState(){
    var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    var wrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');
    var banner = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
    if(!page || !wrap) return;
    var y = wrap.scrollTop || 0;
    var scrolled = y > 48;
    page.classList.toggle('vx-detail-scrolled', scrolled);
    if(banner) banner.classList.toggle('vx-detail-scrolled', scrolled);
  }
  function bindDetailScroll(){
    var wrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');
    if(!wrap || wrap._vxDetailScrollBound) return;
    wrap._vxDetailScrollBound = true;
    wrap.addEventListener('scroll', syncDetailScrolledState, { passive:true });
    syncDetailScrolledState();
  }
  function scheduleBind(){ setTimeout(bindDetailScroll, 0); setTimeout(syncDetailScrolledState, 80); }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', scheduleBind);
  else scheduleBind();
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#dash-priority-list,#anrufe-list,#anrufe-split-left,.mobile-nav')) scheduleBind();
  }, true);

  var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  if(page && window.MutationObserver){
    new MutationObserver(function(){ scheduleBind(); }).observe(page, { attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class','style'] });
  }
  var banner = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
  if(banner && window.MutationObserver){
    new MutationObserver(function(){ syncDetailScrolledState(); }).observe(banner, { childList:true, subtree:true });
  }
})();
</script>
