<script id="vx-state-consistency-guard-v1">
/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════
   VOXERA STATE CONSISTENCY GUARD — v1
   Purpose:
   - Active row marker belongs only to an open detail context.
   - Read/unread actions must not leave stale active rows behind.
   - One UI state = one source of truth. No DB/business logic changes.
═══════════════════════════════════════════ */
(function(){
  'use strict';

  function qsa(sel, root){ return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }
  function str(v){ return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); }

  function getActiveRowSelectors(){
    return [
      '#dash-priority-list .dpr-card.sp-active',
      '#anrufe-list .dpr-card.sp-active',
      '#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card.sp-active',
      '#anrufe-split-right .dpr-card.sp-active',
      '#dash-priority-list .sp-active',
      '#anrufe-list .sp-active',
      '#anrufe-split-left .sp-active'
    ].join(',');
  }

  function getRowSelectors(){
    return [
      '#dash-priority-list .dpr-card',
      '#anrufe-list .dpr-card',
      '#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card',
      '#anrufe-split-right .dpr-card'
    ].join(',');
  }

  function getCardId(card){
    if(!card || !card.dataset) return '';
    return str(card.dataset.id || card.dataset.recordId || card.getAttribute('data-id') || card.getAttribute('data-record-id'));
  }

  function clearActiveRequestRows(reason){
    qsa(getActiveRowSelectors()).forEach(function(el){ el.classList.remove('sp-active'); });
    if(reason && window.VOXERA_DEBUG_STATE) console.debug('[Voxera StateGuard] cleared active rows:', reason);
  }

  function setOnlyActiveRequestRow(id){
    id = str(id);
    if(!id){ clearActiveRequestRows('set-empty'); return; }
    qsa(getRowSelectors()).forEach(function(card){
      card.classList.toggle('sp-active', getCardId(card) === id);
    });
  }

  function getOpenDetailId(){
    var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    var legacyOpen = false;
    if(page){
      legacyOpen = page.classList.contains('is-open') || page.getAttribute('data-vx-display') === 'block' || page.style.display === 'block' || page.style.display === 'flex';
    }

    // Detail-V2 im Desktop-Split verwendet bewusst nicht #call-detail-page.
    // Deshalb darf die alte Guard-Logik die aktive Inbox-Row nicht löschen,
    // solange rechts im Split eine V2-Detailansicht gerendert ist.
    var v2 = document.getElementById('requests-detail-v2');
    var splitRight = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right');
    var splitV2Open = false;
    if(v2 && splitRight){
      var rightVisible = splitRight.offsetParent !== null || splitRight.style.display === 'flex' || splitRight.style.display === 'block';
      var hasDetail = !!String(v2.innerHTML || '').trim();
      var emptyVisible = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-empty');
      var isNotEmptyState = !(emptyVisible && (emptyVisible.style.display === '' || emptyVisible.style.display === 'flex' || emptyVisible.offsetParent !== null) && !hasDetail);
      splitV2Open = rightVisible && hasDetail && isNotEmptyState;
    }

    if(!legacyOpen && !splitV2Open) return '';
    return str((page && page.dataset && (page.dataset.vxDetailId || page.dataset.recordId || page.dataset.id)) || window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || '');
  }

  function syncActiveRows(reason){
    var id = getOpenDetailId();
    if(id) setOnlyActiveRequestRow(id);
    else clearActiveRequestRows(reason || 'no-open-detail');
  }

  // Public helpers for later patches/tests.
  window.vxClearActiveRequestRows = clearActiveRequestRows;
  window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows = syncActiveRows;
  window.vxSetActiveRequestRow = function(id){ setOnlyActiveRequestRow(id); };

  // Wrap detail open: active marker follows the opened detail only.
  var oldShowCallDetail = window.showCallDetail;
  if(typeof oldShowCallDetail === 'function' && !oldShowCallDetail._vxStateGuardWrapped){
    var wrappedShowCallDetail = function(callId){
      var result = oldShowCallDetail.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var id = str(callId || getOpenDetailId());
        if(id) setOnlyActiveRequestRow(id);
        else syncActiveRows('show-detail-no-id');
      }, 0);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedShowCallDetail._vxStateGuardWrapped = true;
    window.showCallDetail = wrappedShowCallDetail;
  }

  var oldShowTaskDetail = window.showTaskDetail;
  if(typeof oldShowTaskDetail === 'function' && !oldShowTaskDetail._vxStateGuardWrapped){
    var wrappedShowTaskDetail = function(taskId){
      var result = oldShowTaskDetail.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var id = str(taskId || getOpenDetailId());
        if(id) setOnlyActiveRequestRow(id);
        else syncActiveRows('show-task-no-id');
      }, 0);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedShowTaskDetail._vxStateGuardWrapped = true;
    window.showTaskDetail = wrappedShowTaskDetail;
  }

  // Wrap detail close/force close: no open detail = no selected row.
  ['vxForceCloseCallDetail','closeCallDetail','hideCallDetail','vxSplitClearDetail'].forEach(function(fnName){
    var oldFn = window[fnName];
    if(typeof oldFn === 'function' && !oldFn._vxStateGuardWrapped){
      var wrapped = function(){
        var result = oldFn.apply(this, arguments);
        setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows(fnName); }, 0);
        return result;
      };
      wrapped._vxStateGuardWrapped = true;
      window[fnName] = wrapped;
    }
  });

  // Read/unread are list state changes, not selection state changes.
  // After read/unread, keep active marker only if that same detail is still open.
  ['vxMarkRequestAsRead','vxMarkRequestAsUnread'].forEach(function(fnName){
    var oldFn = window[fnName];
    if(typeof oldFn === 'function' && !oldFn._vxStateGuardWrapped){
      var wrapped = function(recordId){
        var openIdBefore = getOpenDetailId();
        var result = oldFn.apply(this, arguments);
        var after = function(){
          var openId = getOpenDetailId();
          if(openId) setOnlyActiveRequestRow(openId);
          else clearActiveRequestRows(fnName + ':no-open-detail');
        };
        if(result && typeof result.then === 'function') result.finally(function(){ setTimeout(after, 0); });
        else setTimeout(after, 0);
        return result;
      };
      wrapped._vxStateGuardWrapped = true;
      window[fnName] = wrapped;
    }
  });

  // dprAction sometimes renders immediately after mark-read/unread.
  // Normalize the visual selection after the render cycle.
  var oldDprAction = window.dprAction;
  if(typeof oldDprAction === 'function' && !oldDprAction._vxStateGuardWrapped){
    var wrappedDprAction = function(action){
      var result = oldDprAction.apply(this, arguments);
      if(action === 'mark-read' || action === 'mark-unread') {
        setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('dprAction:' + action); }, 0);
        setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('dprAction:' + action + ':after-render'); }, 80);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedDprAction._vxStateGuardWrapped = true;
    window.dprAction = wrappedDprAction;
  }

  // If a read/unread menu button is clicked directly, normalize after bubbling/rendering.
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-action="mark-read"],[data-action="mark-unread"]');
    if(!btn) return;
    setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('read-toggle-click'); }, 0);
    setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('read-toggle-click:after-render'); }, 100);
  }, true);

  // Keep state correct after tab changes and browser navigation.
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    if(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.mobile-nav-btn,.nav-item,[data-tab]')) {
      setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('nav-click'); }, 80);
    }
  }, true);
  window.addEventListener('popstate', function(){ setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('popstate'); }, 80); });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function(){ setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('hashchange'); }, 80); });

  // Mutation observer: if the detail shell closes via class/style changes, clear stale selection.
  function bindDetailObserver(){
    var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    if(!page || page._vxStateGuardObserved || !window.MutationObserver) return;
    page._vxStateGuardObserved = true;
    new MutationObserver(function(){ syncActiveRows('detail-mutation'); }).observe(page, { attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class','style','data-vx-display','data-vx-detail-id'] });
  }
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', bindDetailObserver);
  else bindDetailObserver();

  // Initial normalization.
  setTimeout(function(){ syncActiveRows('initial'); }, 120);
})();
</script>


<script>
/* VOXERA — Done/read-only UI guard
   Prevents duplicate “Erledigt” actions after a record is already completed. */
(function(){
  if (window.__VX_DONE_READONLY_GUARD__) return;
  window.__VX_DONE_READONLY_GUARD__ = true;

  function currentDetailRecord(){
    var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    var id = String((window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || (page && page.dataset && page.dataset.vxDetailId) || '')).trim();
    if (!id) return null;
    return (typeof window.vxResolveActionRecord === 'function') ? window.vxResolveActionRecord(id) : null;
  }
  function setBtnReadonly(btn, label){
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-disabled','true');
    btn.classList.add('vx-readonly-action');
    btn.classList.remove('is-loading');
    btn.title = 'Dieser Eintrag ist bereits erledigt';
    btn.onclick = function(ev){ if(ev) ev.stopPropagation(); return false; };
    var span = btn.querySelector('span');
    if (span && label) span.textContent = label;
  }
  function hideMenuDoneActions(){
    var menu = document.getElementById('vxd-menu');
    if (!menu) return;
    menu.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(btn){
      var txt = String(btn.textContent || '').toLowerCase();
      if (txt.indexOf('erledigt') >= 0 || txt.indexOf('follow-up') >= 0 || txt.indexOf('fälligkeit') >= 0 || txt.indexOf('faelligkeit') >= 0) {
        btn.style.display = 'none';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      }
    });
  }
  function applyDoneReadonly(){
    var rec = currentDetailRecord();
    if (!(typeof window.vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord === 'function' && window.vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord(rec))) return;
    setBtnReadonly(document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done'), 'Erledigt');
    setBtnReadonly(document.getElementById('vx-cd-bb-due'), 'Erledigt');
    hideMenuDoneActions();
    var content = document.getElementById('call-detail-content');
    if (content && !content.querySelector('[data-vx-readonly-note="done"]')) {
      var note = document.createElement('div');
      note.setAttribute('data-vx-readonly-note','done');
      note.style.cssText = 'margin:0 0 12px;padding:10px 12px;border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:var(--surface2);font-size:12px;color:var(--slate);line-height:1.45;';
      note.textContent = 'Dieser Eintrag ist erledigt. Operative Aktionen sind gesperrt.';
      content.insertBefore(note, content.firstChild);
    }
  }
  var pending = false;
  function schedule(){
    if (pending) return;
    pending = true;
    setTimeout(function(){ pending = false; applyDoneReadonly(); }, 30);
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', schedule, true);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', schedule, {passive:true});
  var oldRenderCall = window.renderCallDetailPage;
  if (typeof oldRenderCall === 'function' && !oldRenderCall._vxDoneReadonlyWrapped) {
    var wrappedCall = function(){ var res = oldRenderCall.apply(this, arguments); schedule(); return res; };
    wrappedCall._vxDoneReadonlyWrapped = true;
    window.renderCallDetailPage = wrappedCall;
  }
  var oldRenderTask = window.renderTaskDetailPage;
  if (typeof oldRenderTask === 'function' && !oldRenderTask._vxDoneReadonlyWrapped) {
    var wrappedTask = function(){ var res = oldRenderTask.apply(this, arguments); schedule(); return res; };
    wrappedTask._vxDoneReadonlyWrapped = true;
    window.renderTaskDetailPage = wrappedTask;
  }
  var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  if (page && window.MutationObserver) {
    new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(page, {childList:true, subtree:true, attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class','style','data-vx-detail-id']});
  }
  window.vxApplyDoneReadonly = applyDoneReadonly;
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-row-state-followup-read-guard">
/* v5.9 — Read/unread contract.
   Ungelesen is an inbox signal only. Once a follow-up/planned state exists, read/unread no longer drives UI. */
(function(){
  if (window.__VX_ROW_STATE_FOLLOWUP_READ_GUARD__) return;
  window.__VX_ROW_STATE_FOLLOWUP_READ_GUARD__ = true;

  function qsa(sel, root){ return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }

  function normalizeRenderedUnreadState(){
    qsa('#anrufe-list .dpr-card[data-filter-planned="1"], #dash-priority-list .dpr-card[data-rail="planned"]').forEach(function(card){
      card.setAttribute('data-filter-new','0');
      qsa('.is-unread', card).forEach(function(row){ row.classList.remove('is-unread'); });
    });
  }

  var oldRenderAnrufe = window.renderAnrufe;
  if (typeof oldRenderAnrufe === 'function' && !oldRenderAnrufe._vxFollowupReadWrapped) {
    window.renderAnrufe = function(){
      var result = oldRenderAnrufe.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(normalizeRenderedUnreadState, 0);
      setTimeout(normalizeRenderedUnreadState, 80);
      return result;
    };
    window.renderAnrufe._vxFollowupReadWrapped = true;
  }

  var oldMarkUnread = window.vxMarkRequestAsUnread;
  if (typeof oldMarkUnread === 'function' && !oldMarkUnread._vxFollowupReadWrapped) {
    window.vxMarkRequestAsUnread = function(recordId){
      var rec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId) : null;
      if (typeof vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord === 'function' && !vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord(rec)) {
        if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Ungelesen ist nur für neue eingehende Anrufe relevant.', 'info');
        normalizeRenderedUnreadState();
        return Promise.resolve(false);
      }
      return oldMarkUnread.apply(this, arguments);
    };
    window.vxMarkRequestAsUnread._vxFollowupReadWrapped = true;
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-action="mark-unread"]');
    if (!btn) return;
    var recId = (btn.dataset && (btn.dataset.recId || btn.dataset.id)) || '';
    var rec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(recId) : null;
    if (typeof vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord === 'function' && !vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord(rec)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Ungelesen ist nur für neue eingehende Anrufe relevant.', 'info');
      normalizeRenderedUnreadState();
    }
  }, true);

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', normalizeRenderedUnreadState);
  else normalizeRenderedUnreadState();
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-active-selection-split-v2-final-2026-05-23">
/* PR-FIX 2026-05-23 — robuste aktive Anfrage-Selection.
   Root Cause: alte State-Guards bewerteten Detail-V2 im Split als "geschlossen",
   weil #call-detail-page dort absichtlich display:none ist. Dadurch wurde sp-active
   nach dem Klick wieder entfernt. Diese finale Schicht nutzt die geöffnete Record-ID
   als Source of Truth, nicht die Sichtbarkeit des Legacy-Detail-Panels. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_ACTIVE_SELECTION_SPLIT_V2_FINAL__) return;
  window.__VX_ACTIVE_SELECTION_SPLIT_V2_FINAL__ = true;

  function str(v){ return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); }
  function qsa(sel, root){ return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }

  function rowId(el){
    if(!el) return '';
    var card = el.closest && el.closest('.dpr-card');
    var row = el.closest && el.closest('.vx-heute-row,.vx-anf-row,.dpr-row');
    return str(
      (card && card.dataset && (card.dataset.id || card.dataset.recordId)) ||
      (row && row.dataset && (row.dataset.id || row.dataset.recordId)) ||
      (el.dataset && (el.dataset.id || el.dataset.recordId)) ||
      ''
    );
  }

  function activeIdFromState(){
    return str(window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || '');
  }

  function applyActive(id){
    id = str(id);
    if(!id) return;
    window._vxActiveRecordId = id;
    window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId = id;

    qsa('#anrufe-list .sp-active,#anrufe-split-left .sp-active').forEach(function(el){
      el.classList.remove('sp-active');
    });

    qsa('#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-list .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-anf-row').forEach(function(el){
      if(rowId(el) === id) el.classList.add('sp-active');
    });
  }

  function scheduleApply(id){
    id = str(id || activeIdFromState());
    if(!id) return;
    applyActive(id);
    [0, 30, 90, 180, 350].forEach(function(ms){
      setTimeout(function(){ applyActive(id); }, ms);
    });
  }

  window.vxSetActiveRequestRow = function(id){ scheduleApply(id); };
  window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows = function(){ scheduleApply(activeIdFromState()); };

  var oldShowCallDetail = window.showCallDetail;
  if(typeof oldShowCallDetail === 'function' && !oldShowCallDetail._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped){
    var wrappedShowCallDetail = function(callId){
      var id = str(callId);
      if(id) applyActive(id);
      var result = oldShowCallDetail.apply(this, arguments);
      if(id) scheduleApply(id);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedShowCallDetail._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped = true;
    window.showCallDetail = wrappedShowCallDetail;
  }

  var oldShowTaskDetail = window.showTaskDetail;
  if(typeof oldShowTaskDetail === 'function' && !oldShowTaskDetail._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped){
    var wrappedShowTaskDetail = function(taskId){
      var id = str(taskId);
      if(id) applyActive(id);
      var result = oldShowTaskDetail.apply(this, arguments);
      if(id) scheduleApply(id);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedShowTaskDetail._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped = true;
    window.showTaskDetail = wrappedShowTaskDetail;
  }

  var oldRenderAnrufe = window.renderAnrufe;
  if(typeof oldRenderAnrufe === 'function' && !oldRenderAnrufe._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped){
    var wrappedRenderAnrufe = function(){
      var result = oldRenderAnrufe.apply(this, arguments);
      scheduleApply(activeIdFromState());
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRenderAnrufe._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped = true;
    window.renderAnrufe = wrappedRenderAnrufe;
  }

  var oldRefreshRead = window.vxRefreshAfterReadStateChange;
  if(typeof oldRefreshRead === 'function' && !oldRefreshRead._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped){
    var wrappedRefreshRead = function(){
      var id = activeIdFromState();
      var result = oldRefreshRead.apply(this, arguments);
      if(id) scheduleApply(id);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRefreshRead._vxSplitV2FinalWrapped = true;
    window.vxRefreshAfterReadStateChange = wrappedRefreshRead;
  }

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    var row = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-list .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-anf-row');
    if(!row || (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('button,.vx-row-menu,.vx-ibtn'))) return;
    var id = rowId(row);
    if(id) scheduleApply(id);
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var row = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-list .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-anf-row');
    if(!row || (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('button,.vx-row-menu,.vx-ibtn'))) return;
    var id = rowId(row);
    if(id) scheduleApply(id);
  }, true);

  function observeList(id){
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el || !window.MutationObserver || el._vxActiveSelectionObserved) return;
    el._vxActiveSelectionObserved = true;
    new MutationObserver(function(){ scheduleApply(activeIdFromState()); }).observe(el, { childList:true, subtree:true });
  }

  function boot(){
    observeList('anrufe-list');
    observeList('anrufe-split-left');
    scheduleApply(activeIdFromState());
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();

<script id="vx-row-stabilisierung-v3-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_ROW_STABILISIERUNG_V3__) return;
  window.__VX_ROW_STABILISIERUNG_V3__ = true;

  function str(v){ return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); }
  function qsa(sel, root){ return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }
  function rowId(el){
    if(!el) return '';
    var card = el.closest && el.closest('.dpr-card');
    var row = el.closest && el.closest('.vx-heute-row,.vx-anf-row,.dpr-row');
    return str((card && card.dataset && (card.dataset.id || card.dataset.recordId)) || (row && row.dataset && (row.dataset.id || row.dataset.recordId)) || (el.dataset && (el.dataset.id || el.dataset.recordId)) || '');
  }
  function applyActive(id){
    id = str(id);
    if(!id) return;
    window._vxActiveRecordId = id;
    window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId = id;
    qsa('#anrufe-list .sp-active,#anrufe-split-left .sp-active').forEach(function(el){ el.classList.remove('sp-active'); });
    qsa('#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-list .vx-anf-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-anf-row').forEach(function(el){
      if(rowId(el) === id) el.classList.add('sp-active');
    });
  }
  window.vxSetActiveRequestRow = applyActive;
  window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows = function(){ applyActive(window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || ''); };

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    var row = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#anrufe-list .dpr-card,#anrufe-split-left .dpr-card,#anrufe-list .vx-heute-row,#anrufe-split-left .vx-heute-row');
    if(!row || (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('button,.vx-row-menu,.vx-ibtn'))) return;
    var id = rowId(row);
    if(id) applyActive(id);
  }, true);

  function ensureFilterBanner(){
    var subWrap = document.getElementById('anrufe-inbox-subfilters');
    if(!subWrap) return null;
    var banner = document.getElementById('anrufe-active-filter-banner');
    if(!banner){
      banner = document.createElement('div');
      banner.id = 'anrufe-active-filter-banner';
      banner.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-bold ph-funnel" aria-hidden="true"></i><span></span><button type="button" aria-label="Filter entfernen">×</button>';
      subWrap.parentNode.insertBefore(banner, subWrap.nextSibling);
      var btn = banner.querySelector('button');
      if(btn) btn.onclick = function(){
        window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly = false;
        if(typeof _anrufeInboxSubfilter !== 'undefined') _anrufeInboxSubfilter = 'all';
        if(typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
      };
    }
    return banner;
  }
  window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner = function(){
    var banner = ensureFilterBanner();
    if(!banner) return;
    var span = banner.querySelector('span');
    var count = qsa('#anrufe-list .dpr-card').length;
    var text = '';
    if(window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly){ text = 'Filter: Heute · ' + count + ' ' + (count === 1 ? 'Eintrag' : 'Einträge'); }
    else if(typeof _anrufeInboxSubfilter !== 'undefined' && _anrufeInboxSubfilter === 'unread'){ text = 'Filter: Nur ungelesene · ' + count + ' ' + (count === 1 ? 'Eintrag' : 'Einträge'); }
    if(text){
      if(span) span.textContent = text;
      banner.classList.add('is-visible');
    } else {
      banner.classList.remove('is-visible');
    }
  };

  var oldRender = window.renderAnrufe;
  if(typeof oldRender === 'function' && !oldRender._vxRowStableV3Wrapped){
    var wrapped = function(){
      var result = oldRender.apply(this, arguments);
      window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner();
      window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped._vxRowStableV3Wrapped = true;
    window.renderAnrufe = wrapped;
  }

  function boot(){
    ensureFilterBanner();
    window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner();
    window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows();
  }
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-pr-h-inbox-row-final-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_PR_H_INBOX_FINAL__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_H_INBOX_FINAL__ = true;

  function cleanRowMenus(root){
    root = root || document;
    var menus = root.querySelectorAll('#anrufe-list .vx-row-menu, #anrufe-split-left .vx-row-menu, #vx-row-menu-portal');
    menus.forEach(function(menu){
      var seenReadToggle = false;
      Array.prototype.slice.call(menu.querySelectorAll('button')).forEach(function(btn){
        var action = (btn.dataset && btn.dataset.action) || '';
        var text = (btn.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        var remove = false;
        if (action === 'fulldetail' || action === 'copy-details' || action === 'done' || action === 'reopen') remove = true;
        if (text === 'details öffnen' || text === 'details kopieren' || text === 'als erledigt markieren' || text === 'wieder öffnen') remove = true;
        if (action === 'mark-read' || action === 'mark-unread') {
          if (seenReadToggle) remove = true;
          seenReadToggle = true;
        }
        if (remove && btn.parentNode) btn.parentNode.removeChild(btn);
      });
    });
  }

  var oldOpenFloating = window.vxOpenFloatingRowMenu;
  if (typeof oldOpenFloating === 'function' && !oldOpenFloating._vxPrHWrapped) {
    var wrapped = function(){
      var result = oldOpenFloating.apply(this, arguments);
      cleanRowMenus(document);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped._vxPrHWrapped = true;
    window.vxOpenFloatingRowMenu = wrapped;
  }
  var oldOpenFloatingAt = window.vxOpenFloatingRowMenuAtPoint;
  if (typeof oldOpenFloatingAt === 'function' && !oldOpenFloatingAt._vxPrHWrapped) {
    var wrappedAt = function(){
      var result = oldOpenFloatingAt.apply(this, arguments);
      cleanRowMenus(document);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedAt._vxPrHWrapped = true;
    window.vxOpenFloatingRowMenuAtPoint = wrappedAt;
  }

  var oldRender = window.renderAnrufe;
  if (typeof oldRender === 'function' && !oldRender._vxPrHWrapped) {
    var wrappedRender = function(){
      var result = oldRender.apply(this, arguments);
      cleanRowMenus(document);
      if (typeof window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows === 'function') window.vxSyncActiveRequestRows();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRender._vxPrHWrapped = true;
    window.renderAnrufe = wrappedRender;
  }

  function boot(){ cleanRowMenus(document); }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();
</script>



<script id="vx-pr-i-unread-menu-normalizer-2026-05-23">
/* PR-I — Read/unread cleanup.
   Keeps legacy/floating menus from showing duplicated read actions and prevents read toggles from clearing active row. */
(function(){
  if (window.__VX_PR_I_UNREAD_MENU_NORMALIZER__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_I_UNREAD_MENU_NORMALIZER__ = true;

  function normalizeMenu(menu) {
    if (!menu) return;
    var seenReadToggle = false;
    Array.prototype.slice.call(menu.querySelectorAll('button')).forEach(function(btn){
      var action = btn.getAttribute('data-action') || '';
      var label = String(btn.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var remove = false;
      if (action === 'fulldetail' || label === 'details öffnen') remove = true;
      if (action === 'copy-details' || label === 'details kopieren') remove = true;
      if (action === 'done' || label === 'als erledigt markieren') remove = true;
      if (label === 'wieder öffnen') remove = true;
      if (action === 'mark-read' || action === 'mark-unread') {
        if (seenReadToggle) remove = true;
        seenReadToggle = true;
      }
      if (remove) btn.remove();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var trigger = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-action="overflow"], .vx-ibtn.is-overflow');
    if (!trigger) return;
    setTimeout(function(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.vx-row-menu, #vx-ctx-menu').forEach(normalizeMenu);
    }, 0);
  }, true);

  var oldDprAction = window.dprAction;
  if (typeof oldDprAction === 'function' && !oldDprAction._vxPrIUnreadWrapped) {
    window.dprAction = function(action, recId, extra){
      var activeBefore = String(window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || recId || '');
      var result = oldDprAction.apply(this, arguments);
      if (action === 'mark-read' || action === 'mark-unread') {
        setTimeout(function(){
          if (activeBefore && typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(activeBefore);
        }, 0);
      }
      return result;
    };
    window.dprAction._vxPrIUnreadWrapped = true;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-pr-i2-unread-source-of-truth-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_PR_I2_UNREAD_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_I2_UNREAD_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH__ = true;

  function str(v){ return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); }
  function lower(v){ return str(v).toLowerCase(); }
  function hasOwn(obj,key){ return !!obj && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj,key); }
  function field(record,key){ var f=(record&&record.fields)||{}; return hasOwn(record,key)?record[key]:f[key]; }
  function parseBool(value){
    if (value === true || value === 1) return true;
    if (value === false || value === 0) return false;
    if (value == null) return null;
    var s = lower(value);
    if (['true','1','yes','ja','y'].indexOf(s)!==-1) return true;
    if (['false','0','no','nein','n',''].indexOf(s)!==-1) return false;
    return null;
  }
  function normalizeStatus(value){
    if (typeof window.vxNormalizeDashboardStatus === 'function') return window.vxNormalizeDashboardStatus(value);
    var raw = lower(value);
    if (!raw) return '';
    if (['done','completed','complete','erledigt','abgeschlossen','archived','archiviert','closed','resolved','cancelled','deleted'].indexOf(raw)!==-1) return 'done';
    if (['planned','geplant','followup','follow_up','scheduled','callback_planned','follow_up_scheduled'].indexOf(raw)!==-1) return 'planned';
    if (['new','neu','open','offen','pending','in_progress','in bearbeitung','processing'].indexOf(raw)!==-1) return 'open';
    return raw;
  }
  function recordId(record){
    var f=(record&&record.fields)||{};
    return str((record&&(record.id||record.call_id||record.task_id)) || f.id || f.call_id || f.task_id || '');
  }
  function isDoneOrArchived(record){
    var f=(record&&record.fields)||{};
    if (!record) return false;
    var hardDates = ['archived_at','completed_at','deleted_at','closed_at','resolved_at'];
    for (var i=0;i<hardDates.length;i++){
      var v = (hasOwn(record, hardDates[i]) ? record[hardDates[i]] : f[hardDates[i]]);
      if (v != null && str(v) !== '') return true;
    }
    var statusFields = ['dashboard_status','status','live_status','call_status','case_status'];
    for (var j=0;j<statusFields.length;j++){
      var n = normalizeStatus(hasOwn(record,statusFields[j]) ? record[statusFields[j]] : f[statusFields[j]]);
      if (n === 'done') return true;
    }
    try { if (typeof window.vxIsDoneRecord === 'function' && window.vxIsDoneRecord(record)) return true; } catch(e) {}
    return false;
  }
  function isPlanned(record){
    if (!record || isDoneOrArchived(record)) return false;
    var f=(record&&record.fields)||{};
    var statusFields = ['dashboard_status','status','live_status','call_status','case_status'];
    for (var j=0;j<statusFields.length;j++){
      var n = normalizeStatus(hasOwn(record,statusFields[j]) ? record[statusFields[j]] : f[statusFields[j]]);
      if (n === 'planned') return true;
    }
    try { if (typeof window.vxHasPlannedNextAction === 'function' && window.vxHasPlannedNextAction(record)) return true; } catch(e) {}
    return false;
  }

  window.vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord = function(record){
    if (!record) return false;
    try { if (typeof window.isManualTaskRecord === 'function' && window.isManualTaskRecord(record)) return false; } catch(e) {}
    if (isDoneOrArchived(record)) return false;
    if (isPlanned(record)) return false;
    return true;
  };

  window.vxIsUnreadRecord = function(record){
    if (!window.vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord(record)) return false;
    var f=(record&&record.fields)||{};
    var id=recordId(record);
    try { if (id && typeof window.vxGetManualUnreadIdSet === 'function' && window.vxGetManualUnreadIdSet().has(id)) return true; } catch(e) {}

    var candidates=[];
    ['is_unread','unread'].forEach(function(k){
      if (hasOwn(record,k)) candidates.push(record[k]);
      if (hasOwn(f,k)) candidates.push(f[k]);
    });
    for (var i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){
      var parsed=parseBool(candidates[i]);
      if (parsed === true) return true;
      if (parsed === false) return false;
    }

    // read_at leer zählt nur bei offenen, eligible Records. Erledigt/Archiviert wurde oben bereits ausgeschlossen.
    var hasReadAt = hasOwn(record,'read_at') || hasOwn(f,'read_at');
    if (hasReadAt) {
      var readAt = hasOwn(record,'read_at') ? record.read_at : f.read_at;
      if (readAt != null && str(readAt) !== '') return false;
      try { if (id && typeof window.getSeenCallIdsFromStorage === 'function' && window.getSeenCallIdsFromStorage().has(id)) return false; } catch(e) {}
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  function getSource(){
    try { if (typeof window.vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords === 'function') return window.vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() || []; } catch(e) {}
    var calls=[]; var tasks=[];
    try { calls = Array.isArray(window.allRecords) ? window.allRecords : []; } catch(e) {}
    try { tasks = Array.isArray(window.allManualTaskRecords) ? window.allManualTaskRecords : []; } catch(e) {}
    return [].concat(calls,tasks).filter(Boolean);
  }
  function unreadInboxCount(){ return getSource().filter(function(r){ return window.vxIsUnreadRecord(r); }).length; }

  function syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility(){
    var isOpen = (typeof window._anrufeFilter !== 'undefined' ? window._anrufeFilter : 'offen') === 'offen';
    var subWrap = document.getElementById('anrufe-inbox-subfilters');
    if (subWrap) {
      subWrap.style.display = isOpen ? '' : 'none';
      subWrap.dataset.hidden = isOpen ? 'false' : 'true';
    }
    if (!isOpen && typeof window._anrufeInboxSubfilter !== 'undefined') window._anrufeInboxSubfilter = 'all';
  }
  window.vxSyncUnreadSubfilterVisibility = syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility;

  var oldChip = window.anrufeChipFilter;
  if (typeof oldChip === 'function' && !oldChip._vxPrI2Wrapped) {
    window.anrufeChipFilter = function(filter, btn){
      if (filter !== 'offen' && typeof window._anrufeInboxSubfilter !== 'undefined') window._anrufeInboxSubfilter = 'all';
      var res = oldChip.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility, 0);
      return res;
    };
    window.anrufeChipFilter._vxPrI2Wrapped = true;
  }

  var oldRender = window.renderAnrufe;
  if (typeof oldRender === 'function' && !oldRender._vxPrI2Wrapped) {
    window.renderAnrufe = function(){
      syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility();
      var res = oldRender.apply(this, arguments);
      syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility();
      if (typeof window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner === 'function') window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner();
      return res;
    };
    window.renderAnrufe._vxPrI2Wrapped = true;
  }

  var oldBanner = window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner;
  window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner = function(){
    if (typeof oldBanner === 'function') oldBanner.apply(this, arguments);
    var isOpen = (typeof window._anrufeFilter !== 'undefined' ? window._anrufeFilter : 'offen') === 'offen';
    var banner = document.getElementById('anrufe-active-filter-banner');
    if (banner && !window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly && (!isOpen || window._anrufeInboxSubfilter !== 'unread')) banner.classList.remove('is-visible');
  };

  var oldBadges = window.vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges;
  window.vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges = function(){
    var count = unreadInboxCount();
    var label = count > 9 ? '9+' : String(count || 0);
    var badgeA = document.getElementById('badge-anrufe');
    var mb1 = document.getElementById('mnav-badge-anrufe');
    if (badgeA) { badgeA.textContent = label; badgeA.style.display = count > 0 ? '' : 'none'; }
    if (mb1) { mb1.textContent = label; mb1.style.display = count > 0 ? 'block' : 'none'; }
    var subWrap = document.getElementById('anrufe-inbox-subfilters');
    if (subWrap) {
      subWrap.querySelectorAll('[data-inbox-subfilter="unread"]').forEach(function(btn){
        btn.textContent = 'Nur ungelesene' + (count ? ' (' + count + ')' : '');
        btn.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', window._anrufeInboxSubfilter === 'unread');
      });
    }
  };

  function boot(){
    syncUnreadSubfilterVisibility();
    window.vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges();
    if (typeof window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner === 'function') window.vxUpdateAnfragenActiveFilterBanner();
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();
</script>



<script id="vx-pr-j1-detail-v2-info-expansion-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  function esc(v){
    try { if (typeof _esc === 'function') return _esc(v == null ? '' : String(v)); } catch(e) {}
    return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){ return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c]; });
  }
  function label(v){
    try { if (typeof vxLabel === 'function') return vxLabel(v); } catch(e) {}
    return String(v == null ? '' : v);
  }
  function fmtTs(v){
    try { if (v && typeof fmtDate === 'function') return fmtDate(v); } catch(e) {}
    return v ? String(v) : '';
  }
  function duration(v){
    try { if (v != null && v !== '' && !isNaN(Number(v)) && typeof _formatDuration === 'function') return _formatDuration(v); } catch(e) {}
    if (v == null || v === '' || isNaN(Number(v))) return '';
    var n = Number(v);
    if (n < 60) return Math.round(n) + ' Sek.';
    return Math.floor(n / 60) + ' Min. ' + Math.round(n % 60) + ' Sek.';
  }
  function truthy(v){
    if (v === true) return true;
    var s = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase();
    return ['true','1','yes','ja','y'].indexOf(s) !== -1;
  }
  function firstNonEmpty(){
    for (var i=0;i<arguments.length;i++){
      var v = arguments[i];
      if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== '') return String(v).trim();
    }
    return '';
  }
  function getRecordFields(record){
    return (record && record.fields) ? record.fields : (record || {});
  }
  function getSummary(record, f){
    try { if (typeof vxGetCallSummary === 'function') return String(vxGetCallSummary(record) || '').trim(); } catch(e) {}
    return firstNonEmpty(f.call_summary, f.summary, f.ai_summary, f.description, record && record.summary);
  }
  function getTranscript(f){
    return firstNonEmpty(f.transcript, f.call_transcript, f.conversation_transcript, f.raw_transcript, f.conversation, f.messages_text);
  }
  function isCompleted(record, f){
    try { if (typeof vxInquiryIsCompleted === 'function') return !!vxInquiryIsCompleted(record); } catch(e) {}
    var s = String(firstNonEmpty(f.dashboard_status, f.status, f.state)).toLowerCase();
    return ['erledigt','abgeschlossen','completed','done','archived','archiviert'].indexOf(s) !== -1;
  }
  function isPlanned(record, f){
    try { if (typeof vxInquiryIsPlanned === 'function') return !!vxInquiryIsPlanned(record); } catch(e) {}
    return !!firstNonEmpty(f.followup_at, f.due_at, f.next_action_at);
  }
  function inferActionMeta(record, f){
    var category = String(firstNonEmpty(f.category, f.type)).toLowerCase();
    var summary = String(getSummary(record, f)).toLowerCase();
    var followupAt = firstNonEmpty(f.followup_at, f.due_at, f.next_action_at);
    var callback = truthy(f.callback_requested) || category.indexOf('rückruf') !== -1 || category.indexOf('callback') !== -1;
    var appointment = category.indexOf('termin') !== -1 || summary.indexOf('termin') !== -1;
    var offer = category.indexOf('offerte') !== -1 || category.indexOf('angebot') !== -1 || summary.indexOf('offerte') !== -1 || summary.indexOf('angebot') !== -1;
    var done = isCompleted(record, f);
    if (done) return { tone:'done', title:'Bereits erledigt', text:'Dieser Eintrag ist abgeschlossen. Weitere Schritte sind nur nötig, wenn du ihn wieder aktiv bearbeiten möchtest.', meta:'Status aus dem Eintrag übernommen.' };
    if (followupAt) return { tone:'planned', title:'Rückruf / Aufgabe geplant', text:'Für diesen Eintrag ist ein nächster Zeitpunkt hinterlegt: ' + fmtTs(followupAt) + '.', meta:'Aus followup_at / due_at abgeleitet.' };
    if (callback) return { tone:'callback', title:'Rückruf empfohlen', text:'Der Eintrag enthält ein Rückrufsignal. Prüfe den Kontakt und plane bei Bedarf einen Rückruf.', meta:'Aus callback_requested oder Kategorie erkannt.' };
    if (offer) return { tone:'offer', title:'Offertenanfrage erkannt', text:'Der Eintrag wirkt wie eine Anfrage zu einer Offerte. Prüfe die Zusammenfassung und kontaktiere den Kunden bei Bedarf.', meta:'Aus Kategorie oder Zusammenfassung abgeleitet.' };
    if (appointment) return { tone:'appointment', title:'Terminbezug erkannt', text:'Der Eintrag enthält einen Terminbezug. Prüfe, ob ein Rückruf oder eine Terminbestätigung notwendig ist.', meta:'Aus Kategorie oder Zusammenfassung abgeleitet.' };
    return { tone:'neutral', title:'Kein konkreter nächster Schritt erkannt', text:'Im aktuellen Datensatz ist kein klarer Handlungsbedarf hinterlegt.', meta:'Keine Rückruf-, Termin- oder Offertensignale erkannt.' };
  }
  function chipHtml(text, kind){
    var palette = {
      status:'background:var(--vx-bg);color:var(--vx-text2);border-color:var(--vx-border-md);',
      blue:'background:var(--vx-blue-light);color:var(--vx-blue);border-color:var(--vx-blue-bd);',
      red:'background:var(--vx-red-light);color:var(--vx-red);border-color:var(--vx-red-bd);',
      gold:'background:var(--vx-gold-light);color:#B45309;border-color:var(--vx-gold-bd);',
      green:'background:var(--vx-green-light);color:var(--vx-green);border-color:var(--vx-green-bd);'
    };
    return '<span class="vx-dv2-chip" style="'+(palette[kind]||palette.status)+'">'+esc(text)+'</span>';
  }
  function detailItem(labelText, valueText, subText){
    if (!valueText) valueText = '—';
    var html = '<div class="vx-dv2-info-item"><div class="vx-dv2-info-label">'+esc(labelText)+'</div><div class="vx-dv2-info-value">'+esc(valueText)+'</div>';
    if (subText) html += '<div class="vx-dv2-info-sub">'+esc(subText)+'</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function safeRecordId(record, f){
    return firstNonEmpty(record && record.id, f.id, f.call_id, f.task_id);
  }
  function renderTranscript(transcript){
    if (!transcript) {
      return '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-file-text"></i> Gesprächsverlauf</div><span>Nicht verfügbar</span></div><div class="vx-dv2-muted">Für diesen Eintrag ist kein Gesprächsverlauf im Datensatz vorhanden.</div></div>';
    }
    try { if (typeof vxRenderTranscriptCardHtml === 'function') return vxRenderTranscriptCardHtml(transcript); } catch(e) {}
    return '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-file-text"></i> Gesprächsverlauf</div><span>Rohtext</span></div><div class="vx-dv2-transcript-raw">'+esc(transcript)+'</div></div>';
  }
  function ensureStyles(){
    if (document.getElementById('vx-pr-j1-detail-v2-style')) return;
    var style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = 'vx-pr-j1-detail-v2-style';
    style.textContent = '\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-shell{overflow-y:auto;height:100%;background:var(--vx-bg);}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-header{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;padding:16px 16px 12px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--vx-border);background:var(--vx-bg);}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-avatar{flex-shrink:0;width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--vx-night);color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:600;font-size:15px;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-title{font-weight:600;font-size:15px;color:var(--vx-text);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-phone{color:var(--vx-blue);font-size:13px;margin-top:1px;}\n' +

      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-info-value{font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--vx-text);line-height:1.35;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-info-sub{font-size:11px;color:var(--vx-text3);margin-top:3px;line-height:1.4;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-action-card{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-action-icon{width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--vx-blue-light);color:var(--vx-blue);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-action-title{font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--vx-text);margin-bottom:4px;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-action-text{font-size:13px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--vx-text2);}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-action-meta{font-size:11px;color:var(--vx-text3);margin-top:6px;}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-transcript-raw{white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;color:var(--vx-text2);}\n' +
      '#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-note{width:100%;min-height:90px;padding:10px 12px;border:0.5px solid var(--vx-border-md);border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:inherit;background:var(--vx-bg);color:var(--vx-text);line-height:1.6;}\n' +
      '@media(max-width:1180px){#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-info-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));}}\n' +
      '@media(max-width:760px){#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-info-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;}#requests-detail-v2 .vx-dv2-actions{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2;}}\n';
    style.textContent += style.textContent.replace(/#requests-detail-v2/g, '#archiv-detail-v2');
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }

  window.vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 = function(record, options) {
    options = options || {};
    var host = options.hostEl || document.getElementById('requests-detail-v2');
    if (!host) return;
    ensureStyles();

    var f = getRecordFields(record);
    var name = firstNonEmpty(f.caller_name, f.name, record && record.name);
    var phone = firstNonEmpty(f.phone, f.caller_phone, f.phone_number, f.tel_link, record && record.phone, record && record.caller_phone);
    var rawDate = firstNonEmpty(f.created_at, f.received_at, f.started_at, record && record.created_at);
    var date = fmtTs(rawDate);
    var status = firstNonEmpty(f.status, f.dashboard_status, f.state);
    var category = firstNonEmpty(f.category, f.intent, f.topic);
    var leadQual = firstNonEmpty(f.lead_quality, f.lead_quality_label, f.priority);
    var source = firstNonEmpty(f.source, f.channel, f.direction) || 'Telefonanruf';
    var dur = duration(firstNonEmpty(f.duration_seconds, f.duration, f.call_duration_seconds));
    var summary = getSummary(record, f);
    var note = firstNonEmpty(f.notes_customer_voxera, f.customer_note, f.note, f.notes);
    var transcript = getTranscript(f);
    var done = isCompleted(record, f);
    var planned = isPlanned(record, f);
    var callback = truthy(f.callback_requested);
    var followupAt = firstNonEmpty(f.followup_at, f.due_at, f.next_action_at);
    var actionMeta = inferActionMeta(record, f);
    var displayName = name || phone || 'Unbekannte Nummer';
    var initials = name ? name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(function(w){return (w[0]||'').toUpperCase();}).join('') : (phone ? phone.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-2) : '?');
    var displayMeta = [date, dur].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || '—';

    var leadKind = String(leadQual).toLowerCase() === 'hot' || String(leadQual).toLowerCase() === 'heiss' ? 'red' : (String(leadQual).toLowerCase() === 'warm' ? 'gold' : 'status');
    var chips = [];
    if (status) chips.push(chipHtml(label(status), done ? 'green' : 'status'));
    if (category) chips.push(chipHtml(label(category), 'blue'));
    if (leadQual) chips.push(chipHtml(label(leadQual), leadKind));
    if (planned && followupAt) chips.push(chipHtml('Geplant', 'gold'));
    if (callback) chips.push(chipHtml('Rückruf', 'blue'));

    var html = '';
    html += '<div class="vx-dv2-shell" data-record-id="'+esc(safeRecordId(record, f))+'">';
    html +=   '<div class="vx-dv2-header">';
    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-avatar">'+esc(initials)+'</div>';
    html +=     '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">';
    html +=       '<div class="vx-dv2-title">'+esc(displayName)+'</div>';
    if (phone) html += '<div class="vx-dv2-phone">'+esc(phone)+'</div>';
    html +=       '<div class="vx-dv2-meta">'+esc(displayMeta)+'</div>';
    if (chips.length) html += '<div class="vx-dv2-chips">'+chips.join('')+'</div>';
    html +=     '</div>';
    html +=   '</div>';

    html +=   '<div class="vx-dv2-actions">';
    if (phone) html += '<button class="vx-dv2-btn vx-dv2-btn-primary" onclick="vxCallDetailAnrufen()"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone"></i>Anrufen</button>';
    html +=     '<button class="vx-dv2-btn" onclick="vxCallDetailFaelligkeit()"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-plus"></i>Rückruf planen</button>';
    if (!done) html += '<button class="vx-dv2-btn vx-dv2-btn-done" onclick="vxCallDetailErledigt()"><i class="ph-bold ph-check"></i>Erledigt</button>';
    html +=   '</div>';

    html +=   '<div class="vx-dv2-body">';
    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-sparkle"></i> Anliegen</div><span>Automatisch erstellt</span></div>';
    html +=       summary ? '<div class="vx-dv2-text">'+esc(summary)+'</div>' : '<div class="vx-dv2-muted">Keine Zusammenfassung verfügbar.</div>';
    html +=     '</div>';

    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-action-card"><div class="vx-dv2-action-icon"><i class="ph-bold ph-flag"></i></div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0;"><div class="vx-dv2-action-title">'+esc(actionMeta.title)+'</div><div class="vx-dv2-action-text">'+esc(actionMeta.text)+'</div><div class="vx-dv2-action-meta">'+esc(actionMeta.meta)+'</div></div></div></div>';

    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-info"></i> Anrufdetails</div><span>Übersicht</span></div><div class="vx-dv2-info-grid">';
    html +=       detailItem('Kategorie', category ? label(category) : '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Relevanz', leadQual ? label(leadQual) : 'Nicht bewertet');
    html +=       detailItem('Status', status ? label(status) : (done ? 'Erledigt' : 'Offen'));
    html +=       detailItem('Handlungsbedarf', actionMeta.tone === 'neutral' ? 'Nein' : 'Ja', actionMeta.title);
    html +=       detailItem('Dauer', dur || '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Zeitpunkt', date || '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Quelle', source || '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Rückruf', callback ? 'Ja' : 'Nein', followupAt ? ('Geplant: ' + fmtTs(followupAt)) : '');
    html +=       detailItem('Eintrag', safeRecordId(record, f) ? 'Vorhanden' : 'Ohne ID');
    html +=     '</div></div>';

    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-user"></i> Kontakt</div><span>Stammdaten</span></div><div class="vx-dv2-info-grid">';
    html +=       detailItem('Name', name || '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Telefon', phone || '—');
    html +=       detailItem('Eingang', date || '—');
    html +=     '</div></div>';

    html +=     (typeof vxRenderCallAudioCardHtml === 'function') ? vxRenderCallAudioCardHtml(record) : '';
    html +=     renderTranscript(transcript);

    html +=     '<div class="vx-dv2-card"><div class="vx-dv2-card-head"><div><i class="ph-bold ph-pencil-simple"></i> Persönliche Notiz</div><span>Manuell</span></div>';
    html +=       '<textarea id="vx-dv2-note" class="vx-dv2-note" placeholder="Notiz hinzufügen...">'+esc(note)+'</textarea>';
    html +=       '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;"><button class="vx-dv2-btn vx-dv2-btn-primary" onclick="vxDv2SaveNote()">Speichern</button><span class="vx-dv2-muted">Speichert im aktuellen Eintrag.</span></div>';
    html +=     '</div>';

    html +=   '</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="vx-pr-j4-stability-polish-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_PR_J4_STABILITY_POLISH__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_J4_STABILITY_POLISH__ = true;

  function isClosedStatusJ4(v){
    var s = String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return ['closed','resolved','completed','done','cancelled','archived','deleted','erledigt','archiviert'].indexOf(s) >= 0;
  }
  function getFields(rec){ return (rec && rec.fields) || rec || {}; }
  function getId(rec){ var f=getFields(rec); return String((rec && rec.id) || f.id || f.task_id || f.case_id || f.call_id || '').trim(); }
  function isManual(rec){ try { return typeof isManualTaskRecord === 'function' && isManualTaskRecord(rec); } catch(e) { return false; } }
  function isOpenTaskLike(rec){
    if (!rec) return false;
    var f = getFields(rec);
    if (!isManual(rec) && !String(f.task_entity_id || f.case_id || f.type || '').match(/task|callback|general|appointment|offerte/i)) return false;
    var st = f.status || f.dashboard_status || '';
    return !isClosedStatusJ4(st) && !f.completed_at && !f.archived_at && !f.deleted_at;
  }
  function unique(arr){
    var seen = new Set(), out=[];
    (arr||[]).forEach(function(r){
      var id = getId(r) || JSON.stringify(getFields(r)).slice(0,120);
      if (!id || seen.has(id)) return;
      seen.add(id); out.push(r);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function getOpenTasksUnified(){
    var list=[];
    try { list = list.concat(window.allManualTaskRecords || (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' ? allManualTaskRecords : []) || []); } catch(e) {}
    try { list = list.concat((window.allRecords || (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' ? allRecords : []) || []).filter(isManual)); } catch(e) {}
    return unique(list.filter(isOpenTaskLike));
  }

  /* Eine zentrale, robuste Aufgaben-Zählung für Heute-KPI und Header. */
  window.vxGetOpenTasksUnified = getOpenTasksUnified;
  var prevGetOpenTaskCount = window.getOpenTaskCount;
  window.getOpenTaskCount = getOpenTaskCount = function(){
    var n = getOpenTasksUnified().length;
    if (n > 0) return n;
    return (typeof prevGetOpenTaskCount === 'function') ? prevGetOpenTaskCount() : 0;
  };

  var prevHeuteKpis = window.vxGetHeuteKpiCounts;
  window.vxGetHeuteKpiCounts = vxGetHeuteKpiCounts = function(records, manualTasks){
    var base = (typeof prevHeuteKpis === 'function') ? prevHeuteKpis(records, manualTasks) : {callsToday:0, callbacksOpen:0, importantNow:0, doneToday:0};
    var ops = null;
    try { if (typeof vxGetAnfragenOperationalCounts === 'function') ops = vxGetAnfragenOperationalCounts(records || [], manualTasks || (window.allManualTaskRecords || [])); } catch(e) {}
    var openTaskCount = getOpenTasksUnified().length;
    // Offene Anfragen im Heute-KPI müssen mit dem Anfragen-Tab konsistent sein.
    base.callbacksOpen = ops && typeof ops.inbox === 'number' ? ops.inbox : Math.max(base.callbacksOpen || 0, openTaskCount);
    base.importantNow = Math.max(base.importantNow || 0, openTaskCount);
    return base;
  };

  function syncHeuteKpis(){
    try {
      var recs = window.allRecords || (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' ? allRecords : []) || [];
      var tasks = window.allManualTaskRecords || (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' ? allManualTaskRecords : []) || [];
      var c = window.vxGetHeuteKpiCounts(recs, tasks);
      var kpiTd = document.getElementById('kpi-today-new');
      var kpiDn = document.getElementById('kpi-done-new');
      if (kpiTd) { kpiTd.textContent = String(c.callbacksOpen || 0); kpiTd.style.color = (c.callbacksOpen || 0) > 0 ? '#DC2626' : '#94A3B8'; }
      if (kpiDn) { kpiDn.textContent = String(c.importantNow || 0); kpiDn.style.color = (c.importantNow || 0) > 0 ? '#D97706' : '#94A3B8'; }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  var prevRenderDashboard = window.renderDashboard;
  if (typeof prevRenderDashboard === 'function' && !prevRenderDashboard._vxJ4Wrapped) {
    var wrappedDash = function(){ var r = prevRenderDashboard.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(syncHeuteKpis, 0); return r; };
    wrappedDash._vxJ4Wrapped = true;
    window.renderDashboard = renderDashboard = wrappedDash;
  }

  /* Filter-Reihenfolge: Alle, Offen, Geplant, Erledigt. */
  function normalizeFilterOrder(){
    var wrap = document.querySelector('#anrufe-split-left .vx-split-filters');
    if (!wrap) return;
    var order = ['alle','offen','geplant','abgeschlossen'];
    order.forEach(function(key){
      var btn = wrap.querySelector('button[data-filter="'+key+'"]');
      if (btn) wrap.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }

  function unknownNamePolish(root){
    root = root || document;
    root.querySelectorAll('#anrufe-list .vx-row-name, #anrufe-list .vx-anf-name').forEach(function(el){
      var txt = String(el.textContent || '').trim();
      if (!txt || /^\+?[0-9\s().-]{6,}$/.test(txt) || txt === '—') {
        el.dataset.vxOriginalPhone = txt;
        el.textContent = 'Unbekannter Anrufer';
        el.classList.add('vx-name-fallback');
      }
    });
  }

  function closeAllRowMenus(){
    try { if (typeof vxCloseFloatingRowMenus === 'function') vxCloseFloatingRowMenus(); } catch(e) {}
    try { if (window.vxContextMenu && typeof window.vxContextMenu.close === 'function') window.vxContextMenu.close(); } catch(e) {}
    document.querySelectorAll('#vx-row-menu-portal,.vx-context-menu,.vx-row-menu.is-open').forEach(function(m){
      if (m.id === 'vx-row-menu-portal' || m.classList.contains('vx-context-menu')) m.remove();
      else m.classList.remove('is-open','is-floating');
    });
  }

  // Menüs schliessen bei Klick ausserhalb — Capture-Phase, vor alten Handlern.
  ['pointerdown','mousedown','click','touchstart'].forEach(function(evt){
    document.addEventListener(evt, function(e){
      var t = e.target;
      if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#vx-row-menu-portal') || t.closest('.vx-row-menu') || t.closest('[data-action="overflow"]'))) return;
      closeAllRowMenus();
    }, true);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape') closeAllRowMenus(); }, true);

  var prevRenderAnrufe = window.renderAnrufe;
  if (typeof prevRenderAnrufe === 'function' && !prevRenderAnrufe._vxJ4Wrapped) {
    var wrappedAnrufe = function(){
      var r = prevRenderAnrufe.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){ normalizeFilterOrder(); unknownNamePolish(document); }, 0);
      return r;
    };
    wrappedAnrufe._vxJ4Wrapped = true;
    window.renderAnrufe = renderAnrufe = wrappedAnrufe;
  }

  // Detail-V2 Fallback: wenn Name fehlt, nicht Telefonnummer als Name zeigen.
  var prevDetail = window.vxRenderRequestsDetailV2;
  if (typeof prevDetail === 'function' && !prevDetail._vxJ4Wrapped) {
    var wrappedDetail = function(record, options){
      var f = (record && record.fields) || {};
      var hadName = !!String(f.caller_name || '').trim();
      var r = prevDetail.apply(this, arguments);
      if (!hadName) {
        setTimeout(function(){
          var host = (options && options.hostEl) || document.getElementById('requests-detail-v2');
          if (!host) return;
          var phone = String(f.phone || f.caller_phone || f.tel_link || '').trim();
          host.querySelectorAll('div').forEach(function(el){
            if (String(el.textContent || '').trim() === phone) {
              el.textContent = 'Unbekannter Anrufer';
            }
          });
        }, 0);
      }
      return r;
    };
    wrappedDetail._vxJ4Wrapped = true;
    window.vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 = vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 = wrappedDetail;
  }

  function boot(){ normalizeFilterOrder(); unknownNamePolish(document); syncHeuteKpis(); }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
  setTimeout(boot, 600);
})();
</script>




<!-- PR-M — Status Source of Truth Cleanup: 2026-05-23 -->
<script id="vx-pr-m-status-source-of-truth-cleanup-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_PR_M_STATUS_SOT_CLEANUP__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_M_STATUS_SOT_CLEANUP__ = true;

  function fieldsOf(record){ return (record && record.fields) || {}; }
  function field(record,key){
    var f = fieldsOf(record);
    if (record && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record,key)) return record[key];
    return f[key];
  }

  function cloneForVisualStatus(record){
    if (!record || typeof window.vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus !== 'function') return record;
    var status = window.vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus(record);
    var clone = Object.assign({}, record || {});
    var f = Object.assign({}, fieldsOf(record));
    var dbStatus = status === 'archived' ? 'archiviert' : status === 'done' ? 'erledigt' : status === 'planned' ? 'geplant' : 'neu';
    f.dashboard_status = dbStatus;
    f.status = dbStatus;
    clone.dashboard_status = dbStatus;
    clone.status = dbStatus;
    clone.fields = f;
    return clone;
  }

  // Detail-V2 may still read raw legacy fields internally. Feed it a normalized clone only for display.
  var prevDetail = window.vxRenderRequestsDetailV2;
  if (typeof prevDetail === 'function' && !prevDetail._vxPrMWrapped) {
    var wrapped = function(record, options){
      return prevDetail.call(this, cloneForVisualStatus(record), options || {});
    };
    wrapped._vxPrMWrapped = true;
    window.vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 = vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 = wrapped;
  }

  // Keep existing global helper name, but make it lifecycle-based.
  window.vxGetVisualDashboardStatus = function(record){
    if (typeof window.vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus !== 'function') return field(record,'dashboard_status') || field(record,'status') || 'neu';
    var s = window.vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus(record);
    if (s === 'archived') return 'archiviert';
    if (s === 'done') return 'erledigt';
    if (s === 'planned') return 'geplant';
    return 'neu';
  };

  // Safety refresh after load: recalculates counts and current list using the central status function.
  function boot(){
    try { if (typeof window.vxUpdateAnfragenCount === 'function') window.vxUpdateAnfragenCount(); } catch(e) {}
    try { if (typeof window.renderAnrufe === 'function') window.renderAnrufe(); } catch(e) {}
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){ setTimeout(boot,0); });
  else setTimeout(boot,0);
})();
</script>


<!-- PR-N — Badge/Label Cleanup ohne Renderer-Umbau: 2026-05-23 -->
<script id="vx-pr-n-badge-label-cleanup-2026-05-23">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if (window.__VX_PR_N_BADGE_LABEL_CLEANUP__) return;
  window.__VX_PR_N_BADGE_LABEL_CLEANUP__ = true;

  function esc(v){
    try { if (typeof _esc === 'function') return _esc(v == null ? '' : String(v)); } catch(e) {}
    return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(c){ return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c]; });
  }
